/* === FILE: app_launcher.h === */
#ifndef APP_LAUNCHER_H
#define APP_LAUNCHER_H

#include <windows.h>
#include "types.h"
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <shlwapi.h>
#include "config.h"
#include "helpers.h"
#include "file_search.h"


static void initialize_app_cache() {
    G_APP_COUNT = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < sizeof(ListOfProgramPaths) / sizeof(ListOfProgramPaths[0]); i++) {
        scan_directory_recursive(ListOfProgramPaths[i]);
    }
}

static bool FindAppInCache(const char* name, char* out_path, size_t path_size) {
    for (int i = 0; i < G_APP_COUNT; i++) {
        if (StrStrIA(G_APPS[i].name, name) != NULL) {
            strncpy(out_path, G_APPS[i].path, path_size);
            return true;
        }
    }
    return false;
}

static char* FindAppPathByName(const char* name) {
    for (int i = 0; i < G_APP_COUNT; i++) {
        if (StrStrIA(G_APPS[i].name, name) != NULL) {
            return G_APPS[i].path;
        }
    }
    return NULL;
}

static void launch_app_by_name(const char* display_name) {
    for (int i = 0; i < G_APP_COUNT; i++) {
        if (StrCmpIA(G_APPS[i].name, display_name) == 0) {
            LaunchApp(G_APPS[i].path);
            return;
        }
    }
}

static void launch_app_as_admin(const char* display_name) {
    for (int i = 0; i < G_APP_COUNT; i++) {
        if (StrCmpIA(G_APPS[i].name, display_name) == 0) {
            LaunchAppAsAdmin(G_APPS[i].path);
            return;
        }
    }
}

static void launch_exe_with_args(const char* exe_path, const char* args) {
    char cmd[MAX_PATH * 2 + 512];
    
    if (args != NULL && strlen(args) > 0 && strcmp(args, "0") != 0) {
        snprintf(cmd, sizeof(cmd), "\"%s\" %s", exe_path, args);
    } else {
        snprintf(cmd, sizeof(cmd), "\"%s\"", exe_path);
    }
    
    STARTUPINFOA si = {0};
    si.cb = sizeof(si);
    PROCESS_INFORMATION pi = {0};
    
    if (CreateProcessA(NULL, cmd, NULL, NULL, FALSE, CREATE_NO_WINDOW, NULL, NULL, &si, &pi)) {
        CloseHandle(pi.hProcess);
        CloseHandle(pi.hThread);
    }
}

static void applauncher_enter_callback(const char* selection) {
    if (selection != NULL && strlen(selection) > 0) {
        launch_app_by_name(selection);
    }
}

static void applauncher_ctrlenter_callback(const char* selection) {
    if (selection != NULL && strlen(selection) > 0) {
        printf("TRYING TO LAUNCEH AS ADMING ON %s",selection);
        launch_app_as_admin(selection);
        selection_complete = true;
    }
}

static void applauncher_refresh_callback(const char* filter, HWND hListBox) {
    if (!hListBox) return;

    const char* names[MAX_APPS];
    int count = (G_APP_COUNT < MAX_APPS) ? G_APP_COUNT : MAX_APPS;
    
    for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        names[i] = G_APPS[i].name;
    }
    PopulateListBox(hListBox, filter, names, count, NULL);
}

void TriggerAppLauncher() {
    if (CheckAndBlockIfPopupActive(hPopupWnd)) return;

    if (G_APP_COUNT == 0) initialize_app_cache();

    const char** initial_items = (const char**)malloc(G_APP_COUNT * sizeof(char*));
    if (!initial_items) return;
    
    for (int i = 0; i < G_APP_COUNT; i++) {
        initial_items[i] = G_APPS[i].name;
    }

    PromptUser(initial_items,"🚀 Launcher", G_APP_COUNT, 920, 650,32,
                        applauncher_refresh_callback,
                        applauncher_enter_callback,   
                        applauncher_ctrlenter_callback,
                        NULL,
                        NULL,
                        NULL,
                        NULL);                         
    free(initial_items);
}

#endif


/* === FILE: bindings.h === */
#include "common_ui.h"
#include "config.h"
#include <windows.h>
#include "app_launcher.h"
#include "macro_bindings.h"
#include "vim_motions.h"
#include "window_switcher.h"
#include "vim_bindings.h"
#include "modal.h"
#include "character_sequence.h"

static inline bool CheckVimMotion(int pressed_vk, int modifiers, MotionAction* out_action, const char** out_sequence, int* out_typed_count) {
    extern HWND hPopupWnd;
    extern HWND g_hEditPopup;
    
    if (!g_vim_mode_enabled) return false;
    if (!hPopupWnd || !IsWindow(hPopupWnd)) return false;
    if (!g_ActiveEditControl || !IsWindow(g_ActiveEditControl)) return false;
    if (GetFocus() != g_ActiveEditControl) return false;
    
    bool in_popup = (g_hEditPopup != NULL && IsWindow(g_hEditPopup));
    
    bool shift_held = (modifiers & HOTKEY_MOD_SHIFT) != 0;
    bool ctrl_held = (modifiers & HOTKEY_MOD_CTRL) != 0;
    bool alt_held = (modifiers & HOTKEY_MOD_ALT) != 0;
    
    if (pressed_vk == VK_ESCAPE) {
        if (!IsNormalMode()) {
            SequenceReset();
            *out_action = MOTION_ENTER_NORMAL;
            *out_sequence = NULL;
            *out_typed_count = 0;
            return true;
        }
        return false;
    }
    
    char key_char = (char)pressed_vk;
    char seq_char = tolower(key_char);
    
    bool in_insert = IsInsertMode();
    bool in_normal = IsNormalMode();
    bool in_visual = IsVisualMode();
    
    if (in_insert && SequenceIsWaiting()) {
        if (SequenceTimedOut()) {
            SequenceReset();
            return false;
        }
        
        SequenceAddKey(seq_char, false);
        SequenceStartTimer();
        for (int i = 0; i < VIM_SEQUENCE_BINDING_COUNT; i++) {
            const SequenceBinding* b = &G_VIM_SEQUENCE_BINDINGS[i];
            if (b->mode == MODE_INSERT && SequenceMatches(b->sequence) &&
                ScopeMatches(b->scope, in_popup)) {
                *out_action = b->action_id;
                *out_sequence = b->sequence;
                *out_typed_count = SequenceTypedCount();
                SequenceReset();
                return true;
            }
        }
        
        for (int i = 0; i < VIM_SEQUENCE_BINDING_COUNT; i++) {
            const SequenceBinding* b = &G_VIM_SEQUENCE_BINDINGS[i];
            if (b->mode == MODE_INSERT && SequenceStartsWith(b->sequence) &&
                ScopeMatches(b->scope, in_popup)) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        
        SequenceReset();
        return false;
    }
    
    if (in_insert) {
        
        if (shift_held) {
            return false;
        }
        
        bool is_prefix = false;
        for (int i = 0; i < VIM_SEQUENCE_BINDING_COUNT; i++) {
            const SequenceBinding* b = &G_VIM_SEQUENCE_BINDINGS[i];
            if (b->mode == MODE_INSERT && tolower(b->sequence[0]) == seq_char &&
                ScopeMatches(b->scope, in_popup)) {
                is_prefix = true;
                break;
            }
        }
        
        if (is_prefix) {
            SequenceAddKey(seq_char, true);
            SequenceStartTimer();
            *out_sequence = NULL;
            *out_typed_count = SequenceTypedCount();
            return false;
        }
        
        SequenceReset();
        return false;
    }
    
    if (in_normal) {
        
        if (SequenceTimedOut()) {
            SequenceReset();
            return false;
        }
        
        if (pressed_vk == VK_RETURN || pressed_vk == 160) {
            SequenceReset();
            *out_sequence = NULL;
            *out_typed_count = 0;
            return false;
        }
        
        if (!shift_held) {
            SequenceAddKey(seq_char, true);
            SequenceStartTimer();
            
            for (int i = 0; i < VIM_SEQUENCE_BINDING_COUNT; i++) {
                const SequenceBinding* b = &G_VIM_SEQUENCE_BINDINGS[i];
                if (b->mode == MODE_NORMAL && ScopeMatches(b->scope, in_popup)) {
                    if (SequenceMatches(b->sequence)) {
                        *out_action = b->action_id;
                        *out_sequence = b->sequence;
                        *out_typed_count = SequenceTypedCount();
                        SequenceReset();
                        return true;
                    }
                    if (SequenceStartsWith(b->sequence)) {
                        return true;
                    }
                }
            }
            SequenceReset();
        } else {
        }
        
        for (int i = 0; i < VIM_BINDING_COUNT; i++) {
            const MotionBinding* b = &G_VIM_BINDINGS[i];
            if (b->vk_code == pressed_vk && b->modifiers == modifiers && b->mode == MODE_NORMAL &&
                ScopeMatches(b->scope, in_popup)) {
                *out_action = b->action_id;
                *out_sequence = NULL;
                *out_typed_count = 0;
                return true;
            }
        }
        
        return true;
    }
    
    if (in_visual) {
        return true;
    }
    
    return false;
}

static inline void RemoveSequenceFromEdit(int typed_count) {
    if (!g_ActiveEditControl || !IsWindow(g_ActiveEditControl)) {
        return;
    }
    if (typed_count <= 0) {
        return;
    }
    
    char text[512] = {0};
    UI_GetCurrentText(g_ActiveEditControl,text);
    int text_len = (int)strlen(text);
    
    if (text_len >= typed_count) {
        text[text_len - typed_count] = '\0';
        UI_SetText(g_ActiveEditControl,text);
        UI_SetCursorPosition(g_ActiveEditControl,text_len - typed_count);
    } else {
    }
}

static inline void ExecuteMotion(MotionAction action, const char* sequence, int typed_count) {
    if (typed_count > 0 && sequence != NULL && strlen(sequence) > 0) {
        DeleteLastTypedChar();
    }
    
    for (int i = 0; i < VIM_SEQUENCE_BINDING_COUNT; i++) {
        const SequenceBinding* b = &G_VIM_SEQUENCE_BINDINGS[i];
        if (b->action_id == action && b->handler != NULL) {
            b->handler();
            return;
        }
    }
    
    for (int i = 0; i < VIM_BINDING_COUNT; i++) {
        const MotionBinding* b = &G_VIM_BINDINGS[i];
        if (b->action_id == action && b->handler != NULL) {
            b->handler();
            return;
        }
    }
    
    if (action == MOTION_ENTER_NORMAL) {
        HandleEnterNormal();
    } else if (action == MOTION_ENTER_INSERT_OUT) {
        HandleEnterInsertOut();
    } else if (action == MOTION_ENTER_INSERT_IN) {
        HandleEnterInsertIn();
    } else if (action == MOTION_ENTER_VISUAL) {
        HandleEnterVisual();
    }
}
bool CheckAppHotkeyBindings(int pressed_vk, int modifiers) {
    HWND hActiveWnd = GetForegroundWindow();
    if (!hActiveWnd) return false;

    char activeExe[256] = {0};
    FetchExeNameOnDemand(hActiveWnd, activeExe, sizeof(activeExe) - 1);
    
    for (int i = 0; i < APP_HOTKEY_COUNT; i++) {
        if (G_APP_HOTKEYS[i].vk_code == pressed_vk && 
            G_APP_HOTKEYS[i].modifiers == modifiers) {
            
            if (strcmp(G_APP_HOTKEYS[i].exe_name, "*") == 0 ||
                StrStrIA(activeExe, G_APP_HOTKEYS[i].exe_name) != NULL) {
                
                printf("[APP-HOTKEY] Launching: %s\n", G_APP_HOTKEYS[i].app_name);
                launch_app_by_name(G_APP_HOTKEYS[i].app_name);
                return true;
            }
        }
    }
    return false;
}
void RunMiscApplication(const char* path) {
    if (path == NULL || strlen(path) == 0) return;
    
    
    const char* ext = PathFindExtensionA(path);
    
    
    SHELLEXECUTEINFOA sei = {0};
    sei.cbSize = sizeof(SHELLEXECUTEINFOA);
    sei.fMask = SEE_MASK_FLAG_NO_UI | SEE_MASK_ASYNCOK;
    sei.lpVerb = "open";
    sei.lpFile = path;
    sei.nShow = SW_SHOWNORMAL;
    
    if (StrCmpIA(ext, ".bat") == 0) {
        char dir_path[MAX_PATH];
        strncpy(dir_path, path, sizeof(dir_path) - 1);
        PathRemoveFileSpecA(dir_path);
        
        char cmd[2048];
        
        snprintf(cmd, sizeof(cmd), "cmd /c \"cd /d \"%s\" && \"%s\"\"", dir_path, path);
        system(cmd);
        printf("[MISC] Running .bat in: %s\n", dir_path);
        return;
    }
    
    
    if (StrCmpIA(ext, ".png") == 0 || 
        StrCmpIA(ext, ".jpg") == 0 || 
        StrCmpIA(ext, ".jpeg") == 0 || 
        StrCmpIA(ext, ".bmp") == 0) {
        char cmd[2048];
        snprintf(cmd, sizeof(cmd), "mspaint \"%s\"", path);
        system(cmd);
        printf("[MISC] Opening image with mspaint: %s\n", path);
        return;
    }
    
    
    if (StrCmpIA(ext, ".exe") == 0) {
        sei.lpFile = path;
        if (ShellExecuteExA(&sei)) {
            printf("[MISC] Launched: %s\n", path);
        } else {
            printf("[MISC] Failed to launch: %s (Error: %lu)\n", path, GetLastError());
        }
        return;
    }
    
    
    if (StrCmpIA(ext, ".url") == 0) {
        
        char url[2048] = {0};
        FILE* f = fopen(path, "r");
        if (f) {
            char line[1024];
            while (fgets(line, sizeof(line), f)) {
                if (strncmp(line, "URL=", 4) == 0) {
                    strncpy(url, line + 4, sizeof(url) - 1);
                    url[strcspn(url, "\r\n")] = '\0';
                    break;
                }
            }
            fclose(f);
        }
        if (strlen(url) > 0) {
            ShellExecuteA(NULL, "open", url, NULL, NULL, SW_SHOWNORMAL);
            printf("[MISC] Opening URL: %s\n", url);
        } else {
            printf("[MISC] Failed to read URL from: %s\n", path);
        }
        return;
    }
    
    
    if (StrCmpIA(ext, ".txt") == 0) {
        char cmd[2048];
        snprintf(cmd, sizeof(cmd), "notepad \"%s\"", path);
        system(cmd);
        printf("[MISC] Opening text file: %s\n", path);
        return;
    }
    
    
    if (StrCmpIA(ext, ".lnk") == 0) {
        sei.lpFile = path;
        if (ShellExecuteExA(&sei)) {
            printf("[MISC] Launched shortcut: %s\n", path);
        } else {
            printf("[MISC] Failed to launch shortcut: %s (Error: %lu)\n", path, GetLastError());
        }
        return;
    }
    
    
    if (ShellExecuteExA(&sei)) {
        printf("[MISC] Opened: %s\n", path);
    } else {
        printf("[MISC] Failed to open: %s (Error: %lu)\n", path, GetLastError());
    }
}

bool CheckMiscHotkeyBindings(int pressed_vk, int modifiers) {
    HWND hActiveWnd = GetForegroundWindow();
    if (!hActiveWnd) return false;

    char activeExe[256] = {0};
    FetchExeNameOnDemand(hActiveWnd, activeExe, sizeof(activeExe) - 1);
    
    for (int i = 0; i < MISC_HOTKEY_COUNT; i++) {
        if (G_MISC_HOTKEYS[i].vk_code == pressed_vk && 
            G_MISC_HOTKEYS[i].modifiers == modifiers) {
            
            if (strcmp(G_MISC_HOTKEYS[i].exe_name, "*") == 0 ||
                StrStrIA(activeExe, G_MISC_HOTKEYS[i].exe_name) != NULL) {
              
                RunMiscApplication(G_MISC_HOTKEYS[i].misc_path);
              
                return true;
            }
        }
    }
    return false;
}

void open_browser(const char *url) {
    char command[256];

    snprintf(command, sizeof(command), "start %s", url);

    int result = system(command);
    
    if (result == 0) {
        printf("Browser opened successfully.\n");
    } else {
        printf("Failed to open the browser.\n");
    }
}

bool CheckQuickLinkHotkeyBindings(int pressed_vk, int modifiers) {
    HWND hActiveWnd = GetForegroundWindow();
    if (!hActiveWnd) return false;

    char activeExe[256] = {0};
    FetchExeNameOnDemand(hActiveWnd, activeExe, sizeof(activeExe) - 1);
    
    for (int i = 0; i < QUICKLINK_HOTKEY_COUNT; i++) {
        if (G_QUICKLINK_HOTKEYS[i].vk_code == pressed_vk && 
            G_QUICKLINK_HOTKEYS[i].modifiers == modifiers) {
            if (strcmp(G_QUICKLINK_HOTKEYS[i].exe_name, "*") == 0 ||
                StrStrIA(activeExe, G_QUICKLINK_HOTKEYS[i].exe_name) != NULL) {
              
                open_browser(G_QUICKLINK_HOTKEYS[i].QuickLinkQuery);
              
                return true;
            }
        }
    }
    return false;
}

bool CheckMacroBindings(int pressed_vk, int modifiers, MacroAction* out_action, int* out_delay) {
    HWND hActiveWnd = GetForegroundWindow();
    if (!hActiveWnd) return false;

    char activeExe[256] = {0};
    FetchExeNameOnDemand(hActiveWnd, activeExe, sizeof(activeExe) - 1);
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < MACRO_BINDING_COUNT; i++) {
        if (G_MACRO_BINDINGS[i].vk_code == pressed_vk) {
            if (G_MACRO_BINDINGS[i].modifiers != modifiers) {
                continue;
            }
            
            if (strcmp(G_MACRO_BINDINGS[i].exe_name, "*") == 0) {
                *out_action = G_MACRO_BINDINGS[i].action_id;
                *out_delay = G_MACRO_BINDINGS[i].delay;
                return true;
            }
            
            if (StrStrIA(activeExe, G_MACRO_BINDINGS[i].exe_name) != NULL) {
                *out_action = G_MACRO_BINDINGS[i].action_id;
                *out_delay = G_MACRO_BINDINGS[i].delay;
                return true;
            }
        }
    }
    return false;
}

bool CheckTextSnippetBindings(int pressed_vk, int modifiers, const char** out_text) {
    HWND hActiveWnd = GetForegroundWindow();
    if (!hActiveWnd) return false;

    char activeExe[256] = {0};
    FetchExeNameOnDemand(hActiveWnd, activeExe, sizeof(activeExe) - 1);
    
    for (int i = 0; i < TEXT_SNIPPET_COUNT; i++) {
        if (G_TEXT_SNIPPETS[i].vk_code == pressed_vk && 
            G_TEXT_SNIPPETS[i].modifiers == modifiers) {
            
            if (strcmp(G_TEXT_SNIPPETS[i].exe_name, "*") == 0) {
                *out_text = G_TEXT_SNIPPETS[i].text;
                return true;
            }
            
            if (StrStrIA(activeExe, G_TEXT_SNIPPETS[i].exe_name) != NULL) {
                *out_text = G_TEXT_SNIPPETS[i].text;
                return true;
            }
        }
    }
    return false;
}


/* === FILE: calc.h === */
#ifndef CALC_H
#define CALC_H
#define IDC_INPUT_BOX 101
#define IDC_LIST_BOX 102
#include <windows.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <ctype.h>
#include <stdbool.h>
#include <shlwapi.h>
#include "types.h"
#include "common_ui.h"
#include "helpers.h"

static double g_last_a = 0.0;
static double g_last_b = 0.0;
static char g_last_op = 0;
static bool g_has_last_op = false;
static char g_last_expression[256] = {0};
static double g_flipped_result = 0.0;
static bool g_is_flipped = false;

typedef struct {
    const char* from_unit;
    const char* to_unit;
    double factor;
} UnitConversion;

double parse_number_with_suffix(const char* str) {
    char cleaned[64];
    strncpy(cleaned, str, sizeof(cleaned) - 1);
    cleaned[sizeof(cleaned) - 1] = '\0';
    
    int len = strlen(cleaned);
    while (len > 0 && isspace(cleaned[len - 1])) {
        cleaned[--len] = '\0';
    }
    
    if (len == 0) return 0.0;
    
    char suffix = tolower(cleaned[len - 1]);
    char num_part[64];
    strncpy(num_part, cleaned, len - 1);
    num_part[len - 1] = '\0';
    
    double val = atof(num_part);
    
    if (suffix == 'k') {
        return val * 1000.0;
    } else if (suffix == 'm') {
        return val * 1000000.0;
    } else {
        return atof(cleaned);
    }
}

void format_number_with_suffix(double num, char* out, size_t max_len) {
    if (num >= 1000000.0) {
        snprintf(out, max_len, "%gm", num / 1000000.0);
    } else if (num >= 1000.0) {
        snprintf(out, max_len, "%gk", num / 1000.0);
    } else {
        snprintf(out, max_len, "%g", num);
    }
}

double get_flipped_result() {
    return g_flipped_result;
}

bool is_flipped_mode() {
    return g_is_flipped;
}

void reset_flip_state() {
    g_is_flipped = false;
    g_flipped_result = 0.0;
}

    { "day",     "hour",    24.0 },
    { "d",       "h",       24.0 },

    
    { "hours",   "days",    1.0 / 24.0 },
    { "hour",    "days",    1.0 / 24.0 },
    { "hour",    "day",     1.0 / 24.0 },
    { "hrs",     "days",    1.0 / 24.0 },
    { "hr",      "day",     1.0 / 24.0 },
    { "h",       "d",       1.0 / 24.0 }
};
#define CONVERSION_COUNT (sizeof(G_CONVERSIONS) / sizeof(G_CONVERSIONS[0]))

void trim_trailing_spaces(char* str) {
    int len = strlen(str);
    while (len > 0 && isspace((unsigned char)str[len - 1])) {
        str[--len] = '\0';
    }
}




void live_evaluate(const char* input, char* out_result, size_t max_len) {
    if (!input || strlen(input) == 0) {
        strncpy(out_result, "Waiting for expression...", max_len);
        return;
    }

    char clean[256];
    strncpy(clean, input, sizeof(clean) - 1);
    clean[sizeof(clean) - 1] = '\0';

    
    
    
    char* in_keyword = StrStrIA(clean, " in ");
    if (in_keyword) {
        char target_unit[64] = {0};
        strncpy(target_unit, in_keyword + 4, sizeof(target_unit) - 1);
        trim_trailing_spaces(target_unit);

        *in_keyword = '\0'; 

        double val = 0.0;
        char source_unit[64] = {0};
        if (sscanf(clean, "%lf %63s", &val, source_unit) == 2) {
            for (size_t i = 0; i < CONVERSION_COUNT; i++) {
                if (StrCmpIA(source_unit, G_CONVERSIONS[i].from_unit) == 0 &&
                    StrCmpIA(target_unit, G_CONVERSIONS[i].to_unit) == 0) {
                    snprintf(out_result, max_len, "= %.4g %s", val * G_CONVERSIONS[i].factor, G_CONVERSIONS[i].to_unit);
                    g_has_last_op = false;
                    return;
                }
            }
        }
        strncpy(out_result, "Unknown unit conversion format", max_len);
        g_has_last_op = false;
        return;
    }

    
    
    
    for (int i = 0; clean[i]; i++) {
        if (clean[i] == 'x' || clean[i] == 'X') clean[i] = '*';
    }

    char a_str[64] = {0};
    char op_str[4] = {0};
    char b_str[64] = {0};
    
    if (sscanf(clean, "%63s %3s %63s", a_str, op_str, b_str) == 3) {
        double a = parse_number_with_suffix(a_str);
        double b = parse_number_with_suffix(b_str);
        char op = op_str[0];
        
        g_last_a = a;
        g_last_b = b;
        g_last_op = op;
        g_has_last_op = true;
        strncpy(g_last_expression, clean, sizeof(g_last_expression) - 1);
        
        double result = 0.0;
        
        switch (op) {
            case '+': result = a + b; break;
            case '-': result = a - b; break;
            case '*': result = a * b; break;
            case '/': 
                if (b != 0) {
                    result = a / b;
                } else {
                    strncpy(out_result, "Error: Div by 0", max_len);
                    return;
                }
                break;
            default:
                strncpy(out_result, "Unknown operator", max_len);
                return;
        }
        
        char formatted[64];
        format_number_with_suffix(result, formatted, sizeof(formatted));
        
        snprintf(out_result, max_len, "= %s", formatted);
        return;
    }
    
    
    
    
    double val = parse_number_with_suffix(clean);
    if (val != 0.0 || (strlen(clean) > 0 && clean[0] == '0')) {
        char formatted[64];
        format_number_with_suffix(val, formatted, sizeof(formatted));
        snprintf(out_result, max_len, "= %s", formatted);
        g_has_last_op = false;
        return;
    }
    
    strncpy(out_result, "Parsing input...", max_len);
}




void flip_last_operation(char* out_result, size_t max_len) {
    if (!g_has_last_op) {
        strncpy(out_result, "No operation to flip", max_len);
        return;
    }
    
    if (g_last_b == 0 && g_last_op == '/') {
        strncpy(out_result, "Cannot flip: division by zero", max_len);
        return;
    }
    
    double flipped_a = g_last_b;
    double flipped_b = g_last_a;
    
    double result = 0.0;
    
    switch (g_last_op) {
        case '+': result = flipped_a + flipped_b; break;
        case '-': result = flipped_a - flipped_b; break;
        case '*': result = flipped_a * flipped_b; break;
        case '/': 
            if (flipped_b != 0) {
                result = flipped_a / flipped_b;
            } else {
                strncpy(out_result, "Error: Div by 0 in flipped", max_len);
                return;
            }
            break;
        default:
            strncpy(out_result, "Unknown operator", max_len);
            return;
    }
    
    g_flipped_result = result;
    g_is_flipped = true;
    
    char a_formatted[32], b_formatted[32], result_formatted[32];
    format_number_with_suffix(flipped_a, a_formatted, sizeof(a_formatted));
    format_number_with_suffix(flipped_b, b_formatted, sizeof(b_formatted));
    format_number_with_suffix(result, result_formatted, sizeof(result_formatted));
    
    snprintf(out_result, max_len, "FLIP: %s %c %s = %s", 
             a_formatted, g_last_op, b_formatted, result_formatted);
}




void get_flip_info(char* out, size_t max_len) {
    if (!g_has_last_op || !g_is_flipped) {
        strncpy(out, "Press Tab to flip", max_len);
        return;
    }
    
    char a_formatted[32], b_formatted[32], result_formatted[32];
    format_number_with_suffix(g_last_b, a_formatted, sizeof(a_formatted));
    format_number_with_suffix(g_last_a, b_formatted, sizeof(b_formatted));
    format_number_with_suffix(g_flipped_result, result_formatted, sizeof(result_formatted));
    
    snprintf(out, max_len, "↕ %s %c %s = %s", 
             a_formatted, g_last_op, b_formatted, result_formatted);
}

void get_flip_display(char* out, size_t max_len) {
    if (!g_has_last_op) {
        strncpy(out, "No operation to flip", max_len);
        return;
    }
    
    if (g_last_b == 0 && g_last_op == '/') {
        strncpy(out, "Cannot flip: division by zero", max_len);
        return;
    }
    
    double flipped_a = g_last_b;
    double flipped_b = g_last_a;
    
    double result = 0.0;
    
    switch (g_last_op) {
        case '+': result = flipped_a + flipped_b; break;
        case '-': result = flipped_a - flipped_b; break;
        case '*': result = flipped_a * flipped_b; break;
        case '/': 
            if (flipped_b != 0) {
                result = flipped_a / flipped_b;
            } else {
                strncpy(out, "Error: Div by 0 in flipped", max_len);
                return;
            }
            break;
        default:
            strncpy(out, "Unknown operator", max_len);
            return;
    }
    
    g_flipped_result = result;
    g_is_flipped = true;
    
    char a_formatted[32], b_formatted[32], result_formatted[32];
    format_number_with_suffix(flipped_a, a_formatted, sizeof(a_formatted));
    format_number_with_suffix(flipped_b, b_formatted, sizeof(b_formatted));
    format_number_with_suffix(result, result_formatted, sizeof(result_formatted));
    
    snprintf(out, max_len, "↕ %s %c %s = %s", 
             a_formatted, g_last_op, b_formatted, result_formatted);
}

void get_flip_expression(char* out, size_t max_len) {
    if (!g_has_last_op) {
        strncpy(out, "", max_len);
        return;
    }
    
    char a_formatted[32], b_formatted[32];
    format_number_with_suffix(g_last_b, a_formatted, sizeof(a_formatted));
    format_number_with_suffix(g_last_a, b_formatted, sizeof(b_formatted));
    
    snprintf(out, max_len, "%s %c %s", a_formatted, g_last_op, b_formatted);
}




static void calculator_refresh_callback(const char* filter, HWND hListBox) {
    if (!hListBox) return;
    
    char result_buffer[256] = {0};
    if (filter && strlen(filter) > 0) {
        live_evaluate(filter, result_buffer, sizeof(result_buffer));
    } else {
        strncpy(result_buffer, "Waiting for expression...", sizeof(result_buffer) - 1);
    }
    
    const char* items[1] = { result_buffer };
    PopulateListBox(hListBox, NULL, items, 1, NULL);
}

static void calculator_enter_callback(const char* selection) {
    if (selection && strlen(selection) > 0) {
        char* result = (char*)selection;
        if (strncmp(selection, "= ", 2) == 0) {
            result = (char*)selection + 2;
        }
        CopyToClipboard(result);
    }
}




static void calculator_tab_callback(void) {
    if (!hEditControl) return;
    
    
    char current_input[256] = {0};
    UI_GetCurrentText(hEditControl,current_input);
    
    
    char a_str[64] = {0};
    char op_str[4] = {0};
    char b_str[64] = {0};
    
    if (sscanf(current_input, "%63s %3s %63s", a_str, op_str, b_str) == 3) {
        
        char flipped[256];
        snprintf(flipped, sizeof(flipped), "%s %s %s", b_str, op_str, a_str);
        UI_SetText(hEditControl,flipped);
        
        int len = strlen(flipped);
        UI_SetCursorPosition(hEditControl,len);
        SendMessageA(GetParent(hEditControl), WM_COMMAND, MAKEWPARAM(IDC_INPUT_BOX, EN_CHANGE), (LPARAM)hEditControl);
        
        printf("[CALCULATOR] Flipped: %s\n", flipped);
    } else {
        printf("[CALCULATOR] Cannot flip: not a valid operation\n");
    }
}




static void calculator_ctrlenter_callback(const char* selection) {
    if (!selection || strlen(selection) == 0 || !hEditControl) return;
    
    
    char* result = (char*)selection;
    if (strncmp(selection, "= ", 2) == 0) {
        result = (char*)selection + 2;
    }
    
    
    UI_SetText(hEditControl, result);
    
    
    int len = strlen(result);
    UI_SetCursorPosition(hEditControl, len);
    
    SendMessageA(GetParent(hEditControl), WM_COMMAND, MAKEWPARAM(IDC_INPUT_BOX, EN_CHANGE), (LPARAM)hEditControl);
    
    printf("[CALCULATOR] Result put back: %s\n", result);
}




void TriggerCalculator() {
    if (hPopupWnd != NULL && IsWindow(hPopupWnd)) return;

    const char* default_msg[1] = { "Enter operation (e.g. 5 x 5 or 10 hours in minutes)" };
    PromptUser(default_msg,"🧮 Calculator", 1, 920, 650,32,
                        calculator_refresh_callback,   
                        calculator_enter_callback,     
                        calculator_ctrlenter_callback, 
                        calculator_tab_callback,
                        NULL,
                        NULL,
                        NULL);                         
  
                                                             
}

#endif


/* === FILE: character_sequence.h === */
// character_sequence.h
#ifndef CHARACTER_SEQUENCE_H
#define CHARACTER_SEQUENCE_H

#include <windows.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <stdbool.h>
#include <ctype.h>
#include "common_ui.h"

#define MAX_SEQUENCE_LEN 8
#define SEQUENCE_TIMEOUT_MS 500

typedef struct {
    char keys[MAX_SEQUENCE_LEN];
    int len;
    int typed_count;
    UINT timer_id;
    bool waiting;
    bool timed_out;
} SequenceState;

static SequenceState g_seq_state = {0};

static inline void SequenceAddKey(char key, bool was_typed) {
    if (g_seq_state.len < MAX_SEQUENCE_LEN - 1) {
        g_seq_state.keys[g_seq_state.len++] = key;
        g_seq_state.keys[g_seq_state.len] = '\0';
        g_seq_state.waiting = true;
        g_seq_state.timed_out = false;
        if (was_typed) {
            g_seq_state.typed_count++;
        }
    }
}

static inline void SequenceReset(void) {
    memset(g_seq_state.keys, 0, MAX_SEQUENCE_LEN);
    g_seq_state.len = 0;
    g_seq_state.typed_count = 0;
    g_seq_state.waiting = false;
    g_seq_state.timed_out = false;
    if (g_seq_state.timer_id != 0) {
        KillTimer(NULL, g_seq_state.timer_id);
        g_seq_state.timer_id = 0;
    }
}

static inline const char* SequenceGet(void) {
    return g_seq_state.keys;
}

static inline int SequenceLen(void) {
    return g_seq_state.len;
}

static inline int SequenceTypedCount(void) {
    return g_seq_state.typed_count;
}

static inline bool SequenceMatches(const char* pattern) {
    int seq_len = g_seq_state.len;
    int pat_len = strlen(pattern);
    
    if (seq_len != pat_len) return false;
    if (seq_len == 0) return false;
    
    for (int i = 0; i < seq_len; i++) {
        if (tolower(pattern[i]) != tolower(g_seq_state.keys[i])) {
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

static inline bool SequenceStartsWith(const char* pattern) {
    int seq_len = g_seq_state.len;
    int pat_len = strlen(pattern);
    
    if (seq_len > pat_len) {
        return false;
    }
    if (seq_len == 0) {
        return false;
    }
    
    for (int i = 0; i < seq_len; i++) {
        char pattern_char = tolower(pattern[i]);
        char seq_char = tolower(g_seq_state.keys[i]);
        if (pattern_char != seq_char) {
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

static inline void CALLBACK SequenceTimerProc(HWND hwnd, UINT uMsg, UINT_PTR idEvent, DWORD dwTime) {
    if (g_seq_state.waiting) {
        g_seq_state.waiting = false;
        g_seq_state.timed_out = true;
        printf("[SEQ-DEBUG] Timer expired! Sequence: '%s'\n", g_seq_state.keys);
    }
    g_seq_state.timer_id = 0;
}

static inline void SequenceStartTimer(void) {
    if (g_seq_state.timer_id != 0) {
        KillTimer(NULL, g_seq_state.timer_id);
    }
    g_seq_state.timer_id = SetTimer(NULL, 0, SEQUENCE_TIMEOUT_MS, SequenceTimerProc);
}

static inline bool SequenceIsWaiting(void) {
    return g_seq_state.waiting;
}

static inline bool SequenceTimedOut(void) {
    return g_seq_state.timed_out;
}

static inline void DeleteLastTypedChar(void) {
    printf("[VIM-DEBUG] DeleteLastTypedChar: START\n");
    
    if (!g_ActiveEditControl || !IsWindow(g_ActiveEditControl)) {
        printf("[VIM-DEBUG] DeleteLastTypedChar: g_ActiveEditControl invalid!\n");
        return;
    }
    
    char text[512] = {0};
    UI_GetText(g_ActiveEditControl, text, sizeof(text));
    int len = (int)strlen(text);
    
    DWORD start, end;
    UI_GetCurrentCursorPosition(g_ActiveEditControl, &start, &end);
    int cursor_pos = (int)start;
    
    printf("[VIM-DEBUG] DeleteLastTypedChar: text='%s', len=%d, cursor_pos=%d\n", text, len, cursor_pos);
    
    if (cursor_pos == 0 || len == 0) {
        printf("[VIM-DEBUG] Nothing to delete (cursor at start or empty)\n");
        return;
    }
    
    int delete_pos = cursor_pos - 1;
    char deleted_char = text[delete_pos];
    
    printf("[VIM-DEBUG] Deleting character at position %d: '%c'\n", delete_pos, deleted_char);
    
    memmove(&text[delete_pos], &text[delete_pos + 1], len - delete_pos);
    UI_SetText(g_ActiveEditControl, text);
    
    UI_Edit_SetSel(g_ActiveEditControl, delete_pos, delete_pos);
    
    printf("[VIM-DEBUG] Deleted character '%c', new text='%s', cursor at %d\n", 
           deleted_char, text, delete_pos);
}

#endif 


/* === FILE: common_ui.h === */
#ifndef COMMON_UI_H
#define COMMON_UI_H

#include <sec_api/stdio_s.h>
#include <windows.h>
#include <shlwapi.h>
#include <stdbool.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include "types.h"
#include "config.h"

typedef struct {
    LARGE_INTEGER start;
    LARGE_INTEGER frequency;
} WinTimer;

static inline WinTimer TimerStart() {
    WinTimer t;
    QueryPerformanceFrequency(&t.frequency);
    QueryPerformanceCounter(&t.start);
    return t;
}

static inline void TimerEnd(WinTimer t, const char* operation_name) {
    LARGE_INTEGER end;
    QueryPerformanceCounter(&end);

    double elapsed_us = (double)(end.QuadPart - t.start.QuadPart) * 1000000.0 / (double)t.frequency.QuadPart;

    if (elapsed_us < 1000.0) {
        printf("[ TIMER] %-25s : %8.2f µs\n", operation_name, elapsed_us);
    } else if (elapsed_us < 1000000.0) {
        printf("[ TIMER] %-25s : %8.2f ms\n", operation_name, elapsed_us / 1000.0);
    } else {
        printf("[ TIMER] %-25s : %8.2f s\n", operation_name, elapsed_us / 1000000.0);
    }
}

static inline int UI_GetSelection(HWND hList) {
    int sel = g_SelectedIndex;
    return sel;
}


static inline void UI_ResetContent(HWND hList) {
    g_populated_count = 0;
    SendMessageA(hList, LB_SETCOUNT, 0, 0);
}

static inline void UI_SetSelection(HWND hList, int index, int old_sel) {
    g_SelectedIndex = index;
    
    RECT itemRect;
    if (old_sel >= 0 && old_sel != index) {
        SendMessageA(hList, LB_GETITEMRECT, old_sel, (LPARAM)&itemRect);
        InvalidateRect(hList, &itemRect, FALSE);
    }
    if (index >= 0 && index != old_sel) {
        SendMessageA(hList, LB_GETITEMRECT, index, (LPARAM)&itemRect);
        InvalidateRect(hList, &itemRect, FALSE);
    }
}

    return g_populated_count;
}

}

}

#include "modal.h"


static inline void PopulateListBox(HWND hListBox, const char* filter, 
                                   const char** items, int item_count,
                                   bool (*filter_match)(const char* item, const char* filter)) {
    if (!hListBox) return;
    
    int current_sel = g_SelectedIndex; 
    char current_text[256] = {0};
    if (current_sel >= 0 && current_sel < g_populated_count) {
        strncpy(current_text, g_populated_cache[current_sel], sizeof(current_text) - 1);
        current_text[sizeof(current_text) - 1] = '\0';
    }
    
    int total_matches = 0;
    char clean_filter[256] = {0};
    if (filter) {
        strncpy(clean_filter, filter, sizeof(clean_filter) - 1);
        clean_filter[sizeof(clean_filter) - 1] = '\0';
    }
    
    // === INCREMENTAL FILTERING ===
    int use_previous = 0;
    if (strlen(clean_filter) > 0 && 
        g_last_items == items && 
        g_last_item_count == item_count &&
        g_history_count > 0) {
        
        char* last_filter = g_filter_history[g_history_count - 1];
        if (strlen(clean_filter) > strlen(last_filter) &&
            strncmp(clean_filter, last_filter, strlen(last_filter)) == 0) {
            
            use_previous = 1;
            printf("[OPTIMIZE] Incremental: '%s' -> '%s'\n", last_filter, clean_filter);
        }
    }
    
    if (use_previous && g_match_count_history[g_history_count - 1] > 0) {
        int prev_count = g_match_count_history[g_history_count - 1];
        int* prev_matches = g_match_history[g_history_count - 1];
        
        for (int i = 0; i < prev_count && total_matches < 4000; i++) {
            int idx = prev_matches[i];
            if (!items[idx]) continue;
            
            bool match = false;
            if (filter_match) {
                match = filter_match(items[idx], clean_filter);
            } else {
                match = (StrStrIA(items[idx], clean_filter) != NULL);
            }
            
            if (match) {
                g_matching_indices[total_matches] = idx;
                total_matches++;
            }
        }
    } else {
        for (int i = 0; i < item_count && total_matches < 4000; i++) {
            if (!items[i]) continue;
            
            bool match = false;
            if (strlen(clean_filter) == 0) {
                match = true;
            } else if (filter_match) {
                match = filter_match(items[i], clean_filter);
            } else {
                match = (StrStrIA(items[i], clean_filter) != NULL);
            }
            
            if (match) {
                g_matching_indices[total_matches] = i;
                total_matches++;
            }
        }
    }
    
    if (strlen(clean_filter) > 0 && g_history_count < 20) {
        strncpy(g_filter_history[g_history_count], clean_filter, sizeof(g_filter_history[0]) - 1);
        g_match_count_history[g_history_count] = total_matches;
        memcpy(g_match_history[g_history_count], g_matching_indices, total_matches * sizeof(int));
        g_history_count++;
        printf("[HISTORY] Saved '%s' (%d matches)\n", clean_filter, total_matches);
    }
    
    if (total_matches == 0 && item_count > 0 && strlen(clean_filter) == 0) {
        int force_count = (item_count < 30) ? item_count : 30;
        for (int i = 0; i < force_count; i++) {
            g_matching_indices[total_matches] = i;
            total_matches++;
        }
        g_history_count = 0;
    }
    
int pool_max = MAX_VISIBLE_ITEMS;
    
    // Cap total matches at pool_max - no virtual scrolling, just show first N
    if (total_matches > pool_max) {
        total_matches = pool_max;
    }
    
    g_populated_count = 0;
    
    for (int i = 0; i < total_matches; i++) {
        int raw_data_index = g_matching_indices[i];
        if (!items[raw_data_index]) continue;
        
        strncpy(g_populated_cache[g_populated_count], items[raw_data_index], 511);
        g_populated_cache[g_populated_count][511] = '\0';
        g_populated_cache[g_populated_count][strcspn(g_populated_cache[g_populated_count], "\r\n")] = '\0';
        g_populated_count++;
    }
    
    UI_LB_SetCount(hListBox, total_matches);
    if (total_matches == 1) {
        UI_SetSelection(hListBox, 0, g_SelectedIndex);
    } else {
        UI_SetSelection(hListBox, -1, g_SelectedIndex);
    }
    if (total_matches == 0) {
      if (hDisplayMessage != NULL) {
        KillTimer(hDisplayMessage, 999);
        DestroyWindow(hDisplayMessage);
        hDisplayMessage = NULL;
      }
    }
    
g_last_items = items;
    g_last_item_count = item_count;
    g_TotalMatches = total_matches;
}

COLORREF GetRainbowColor(float t, float s, float v) {
    float hue = t * 240.0f;
    float h = hue / 60.0f;
    int i = ((int)h) % 6;
    float f = h - (int)h;
    float p = v * (1 - s);
    float q = v * (1 - s * f);
    float t_ = v * (1 - s * (1 - f));
    float r, g, b;
    switch (i) {
        case 0: r = v; g = t_; b = p; break;
        case 1: r = q; g = v; b = p; break;
        case 2: r = p; g = v; b = t_; break;
        case 3: r = p; g = q; b = v; break;
        case 4: r = t_; g = p; b = v; break;
        case 5: r = v; g = p; b = q; break;
    }
    return RGB((BYTE)(r * 255), (BYTE)(g * 255), (BYTE)(b * 255));
}

HFONT CreateCustomNerdFont(int height, const char* face_name) {
    return CreateFontA(
        height, 0, 0, 0, FW_NORMAL,
        FALSE, FALSE, FALSE,
        DEFAULT_CHARSET, OUT_DEFAULT_PRECIS, CLIP_DEFAULT_PRECIS,
        CLEARTYPE_QUALITY, DEFAULT_PITCH | FF_DONTCARE,
        face_name
    );
}

void UpdateTextCaret(HWND hwndEditControl)
{
    int caretWidth = 1;   
    int caretHeight = FontSize; 

    if (IsInsertMode()) {
        caretWidth = 2;   
    } 
    else if (IsNormalMode()) {
        caretWidth = 12;  
    } 
    else {
        caretWidth = 12;  
    }

    static int lastWidth = -1;
    static HWND lastHwnd = NULL;
    
    if (caretWidth != lastWidth || hwndEditControl != lastHwnd) {
        DestroyCaret();
        CreateCaret(hwndEditControl, NULL, caretWidth, caretHeight);
        ShowCaret(hwndEditControl);
        
        SetCaretBlinkTime(INFINITE);
        
        lastWidth = caretWidth;
        lastHwnd = hwndEditControl;
    }
}
POINT ptPrimary = { 0, 0 }; 
void CalculateWindowMetrics(int count, WindowLayout* layout,int height , int width,int item_height) {
    POINT ptPrimary = { 0, 0 }; 
    HMONITOR hMonitor = MonitorFromPoint(ptPrimary, MONITOR_DEFAULTTOPRIMARY);

    MONITORINFO mi = {0};
    mi.cbSize = sizeof(MONITORINFO);
    GetMonitorInfoA(hMonitor, &mi);

    int monitorWidth = mi.rcMonitor.right - mi.rcMonitor.left;
    int monitorHeight = mi.rcMonitor.bottom - mi.rcMonitor.top;
    
    layout->w = 900;          
    layout->item_height = item_height; 

    layout->h = 95 + (count > 0 ? (count * layout->item_height) : 0); 

    if (layout->h < height) layout->h = height;
    if (layout->h > width) layout->h = width; 

    layout->x = mi.rcMonitor.left + (monitorWidth - layout->w) / 2;
    layout->y = mi.rcMonitor.top + (monitorHeight - layout->h) / 2;
}

void CreateChildControls(HWND hwndParent, HFONT hFont, const char* list[], int count, const WindowLayout* layout) {
    HINSTANCE hInstance = (HINSTANCE)GetWindowLongPtrA(hwndParent, GWLP_HINSTANCE);
    
    hEditControl = CreateWindowExA(
        0, "EDIT", "", WS_CHILD | WS_VISIBLE | ES_AUTOHSCROLL,
        20, 50, layout->w - 40, 40, hwndParent, (HMENU)IDC_INPUT_BOX, hInstance, NULL
    );
    if (!hEditControl) return;
    
    g_ActiveEditControl = hEditControl;
    UI_Edit_SetFont(hEditControl, hFont);
    SetWindowSubclass(hEditControl, EditSubclassProc, 0, 0);



    if (count > 0) {

        hListBox = CreateWindowExA(
            0, "LISTBOX", "", 
            WS_CHILD | WS_VISIBLE | LBS_NODATA | LBS_OWNERDRAWFIXED,
            20, 105, layout->w - 40, layout->h - 115, hwndParent, (HMENU)IDC_LIST_BOX, hInstance, NULL
        );

        if (!hListBox) return;
        
        SetWindowSubclass(hListBox, ListBoxSubclassProc, 0, 0);
        UI_LB_SetFont(hListBox, hFont);
        UI_LB_SetItemHeight(hListBox, layout->item_height);

        int max_width = layout->w - 40;
        HDC hdc = GetDC(hListBox);
        if (!hdc) return;
        HFONT hOldFont = (HFONT)SelectObject(hdc, hFont);
        
        g_initial_items = list;        
        g_initial_count = count;
        
        UI_SetItemCount(hListBox, count);
        int countCheck = UI_GetCount(hListBox);
        
        UI_Redraw(hListBox);
        UpdateWindow(hListBox); 
        
        GdiFlush(); 
        
        SelectObject(hdc, hOldFont);
        ReleaseDC(hListBox, hdc);
        UI_SetHorizontalExtent(hListBox, max_width);
        
        g_refresh_callback("", hListBox);
    }
}

void SimpleCreateChildControls(HWND hwndParent, HFONT hFont, const char* string, int count, const WindowLayout* layout) { HINSTANCE hInstance = (HINSTANCE)GetWindowLongPtrA(hwndParent, GWLP_HINSTANCE);
    
    hEditControl = CreateWindowExA(
        0, "EDIT", "", WS_CHILD | WS_VISIBLE | ES_AUTOHSCROLL,
        20, 50, layout->w - 40, 40, hwndParent, (HMENU)IDC_INPUT_BOX, hInstance, NULL
    );
    
    if (!hEditControl) return;
    
    g_ActiveEditControl = hEditControl;
    UI_Edit_SetFont(hEditControl, hFont);
    SetWindowSubclass(hEditControl, SimpleEditSubclassProc, 0, 0);

    if (count > 0) {
        hListBox = CreateWindowExA(
            0, "LISTBOX", "", 
            WS_CHILD | WS_VISIBLE | WS_VSCROLL | LBS_NOSEL, 
            20, 105, layout->w - 40, layout->h - 115, hwndParent, (HMENU)IDC_LIST_BOX, hInstance, NULL
        );

        if (!hListBox) return;
        
        SetWindowSubclass(hListBox, SimpleListBoxSubclassProc, 0, 0);
        UI_LB_SetFont(hListBox, hFont);
        UI_LB_SetItemHeight(hListBox, layout->item_height);

        int max_width = layout->w - 40;
        HDC hdc = GetDC(hListBox);
        if (!hdc) return;
        HFONT hOldFont = (HFONT)SelectObject(hdc, hFont);
        
        SendMessage(hListBox, LB_ADDSTRING, 0, (LPARAM)string);
        SendMessage(hListBox, LB_SETCURSEL, (WPARAM)-1, 0);
        UpdateWindow(hListBox); 
        
        GdiFlush(); 
        
        SelectObject(hdc, hOldFont);
        ReleaseDC(hListBox, hdc);
        
    }
}
void ForceWindowFocus(HWND hwndTarget, HWND hwndInput) {
    ShowWindow(hwndTarget, SW_SHOW);

    SetWindowPos(hwndTarget, HWND_TOPMOST, 0, 0, 0, 0,
                 SWP_NOSIZE | SWP_NOMOVE | SWP_SHOWWINDOW | SWP_ASYNCWINDOWPOS);

    HWND hForeground = GetForegroundWindow();
    DWORD foregroundThreadID = GetWindowThreadProcessId(hForeground, NULL);
    DWORD targetThreadID = GetWindowThreadProcessId(hwndTarget, NULL);
    DWORD currentThreadID = GetCurrentThreadId();

    if (foregroundThreadID != currentThreadID)
        AttachThreadInput(currentThreadID, foregroundThreadID, TRUE);
    if (targetThreadID != currentThreadID)
        AttachThreadInput(currentThreadID, targetThreadID, TRUE);

    SetForegroundWindow(hwndTarget);
    SetActiveWindow(hwndTarget);
    BringWindowToTop(hwndTarget);

    if (hwndInput != NULL) {
        SetFocus(hwndInput);
        UI_Edit_UpdateUIState(hwndInput, UIS_CLEAR, UISF_HIDEFOCUS);
        UI_Edit_SetSel(hwndInput, 0, 0);
    }

    if (foregroundThreadID != currentThreadID)
        AttachThreadInput(currentThreadID, foregroundThreadID, FALSE);
    if (targetThreadID != currentThreadID)
        AttachThreadInput(currentThreadID, targetThreadID, FALSE);
}


static inline bool IsPopupActive(HWND hPopupWnd) {
    return hPopupWnd != NULL && IsWindow(hPopupWnd);
}

static inline bool CheckAndBlockIfPopupActive(HWND hPopupWnd) {
    return IsPopupActive(hPopupWnd);
}
static inline void UI_GetCurrentCursorPosition(HWND hEditControl, DWORD *start, DWORD *end) {
    if (start && end) {
        UI_Edit_GetSel(hEditControl, start, end);
    }
}
static inline void UI_SetCursorPosition(HWND hEditControl,int position) {
    UI_Edit_SetSel(hEditControl, position, position);
}
static inline char* UI_GetCurrentText(HWND hEditControl,char *text) {
   GetWindowTextA(hEditControl, text, sizeof(text));
}
static inline void UI_SetText(HWND hEditControl,char* text) {
  SetWindowTextA(hEditControl,text);
}
static inline int UI_GetText(HWND hEdit, char* buffer, int bufferSize) {
    if (!hEdit || !buffer || bufferSize <= 0) return 0;
    int i = GetWindowTextA(hEdit, buffer, bufferSize);
    return i;
}

/* === FILE: explorer.h === */
#ifndef EXPLORER_H
#define EXPLORER_H

#include <windows.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <shellapi.h>
#include "types.h"
#include "helpers.h"
#include "config.h"

#define FILE_EXPLORER_ZOXIDE 4
#define ExplorerZoxideFile G_FILE_ENTRIES[FILE_EXPLORER_ZOXIDE].mf

static char** g_cached_explorer_commands = NULL;
static int g_cached_explorer_count = 0;


static void RebuildExplorerCache(void) {
    if (g_cached_explorer_commands) {
        for (int i = 0; i < g_cached_explorer_count; i++) {
            free(g_cached_explorer_commands[i]);
        }
        free(g_cached_explorer_commands);
        g_cached_explorer_commands = NULL;
        g_cached_explorer_count = 0;
    }
    
    if (!ExplorerZoxideFile || ExplorerZoxideFile->fileSize == 0) {
        return;
    }
    
    size_t lineCount = 0;
    Line* lines = FileGetLines(ExplorerZoxideFile, &lineCount);
    if (!lines) return;
    
    g_cached_explorer_commands = (char**)malloc(lineCount * sizeof(char*));
    g_cached_explorer_count = 0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < lineCount; i++) {
        size_t len = lines[i].length;
        while (len > 0 && lines[i].ptr[len-1] == '\r') len--;
        
        if (len > 0) {
            char* cmd = (char*)malloc(len + 1);
            memcpy(cmd, lines[i].ptr, len);
            cmd[len] = '\0';
            g_cached_explorer_commands[g_cached_explorer_count++] = cmd;
        }
    }
    
    free(lines);
}

static void explorer_refresh_callback(const char* filter, HWND hListBox) {
    if (!hListBox) return;
    
    if (!g_cached_explorer_commands) {
        RebuildExplorerCache();
    }
    
    PopulateListBox(hListBox, filter, (const char**)g_cached_explorer_commands, g_cached_explorer_count, NULL);
}

static void explorer_enter_callback(const char* selection) {
    int selected_index = LB_ERR;
    if (hListBox != NULL) {
        selected_index = UI_GetSelection(hListBox);
    }
    
    if (selected_index == LB_ERR) {
        
        char input[256] = {0};
        if (hEditControl != NULL) {
            UI_GetText(hEditControl, input, sizeof(input));
            if (strlen(input) > 0) {
                
                if (ExplorerZoxideFile->fileSize > 0) {
                    char lastChar = ExplorerZoxideFile->pBuffer[ExplorerZoxideFile->fileSize - 1];
                    if (lastChar != '\n') {
                        FileAppend(ExplorerZoxideFile, "\n");
                    }
                }

                FileAppend(ExplorerZoxideFile, input);
                FileAppend(ExplorerZoxideFile, "\n");
                RebuildExplorerCache();
                
                char* path = QueryZoxidePath(input);
                if (path) {
                    
                    ShellExecuteA(NULL, "open", "explorer.exe", path, NULL, SW_SHOWNORMAL);
                }
            }
        }
    } else {
        
        char* path = QueryZoxidePath(selection);
        if (path) {
            ShellExecuteA(NULL, "open", "explorer.exe", path, NULL, SW_SHOWNORMAL);
        }
    }
}

static void explorer_edit_callback(const char* selection) {
    char* NewexplorerQuery = EditTextPopup(selection,960,540,1000,300);
    
    if (NewexplorerQuery != NULL) {
        WriteToCacheIndex(g_SelectedIndex,NewexplorerQuery);
    }
    
    ReplaceLineInMappedFile(ExplorerZoxideFile,g_SelectedIndex,NewexplorerQuery);
    
    RebuildExplorerCache();
    
    explorer_refresh_callback("",hListBox);
}

static void TriggerExplorer() {
    if (CheckAndBlockIfPopupActive(hPopupWnd)) return;
    
    if (!g_cached_explorer_commands) {
        RebuildExplorerCache();
    }
    
    const char** list = (const char**)malloc(g_cached_explorer_count * sizeof(char*));
    if (!list) return;
    
    for (int i = 0; i < g_cached_explorer_count; i++) {
        list[i] = g_cached_explorer_commands[i];
    }
    
    ImportantPromptUser(list, "📁 Explorer", g_cached_explorer_count, 920, 650, 32, 
               explorer_refresh_callback, 
               explorer_enter_callback, 
               explorer_edit_callback);
    
    free(list);
}

#endif
