/* === FILE: file_search.h === */
#ifndef FILE_SEARCH_H
#define FILE_SEARCH_H

#include <windows.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <shlwapi.h>
#define MAX_FILES 2000

typedef struct {
    char name[256];
    char path[MAX_PATH];
} FileItem;

static FileItem G_FILES[MAX_FILES];
static int G_FILE_COUNT = 0;

static char* SearchFileInDirectory(const char* directory, const char* search_string) {
    if (!directory || !search_string || strlen(search_string) == 0) {
        return NULL;
    }
    char search_path[MAX_PATH];
    snprintf(search_path, sizeof(search_path), "%s\\*", directory);

    WIN32_FIND_DATAA find_data;
    HANDLE hFind = FindFirstFileA(search_path, &find_data);

    if (hFind == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) {
        return NULL;
    }

    static char found_path[MAX_PATH];  

    do {
        
        if (find_data.dwFileAttributes & FILE_ATTRIBUTE_DIRECTORY) {
            continue;
        }

        
        if (StrStrIA(find_data.cFileName, search_string) != NULL) {
            snprintf(found_path, sizeof(found_path), "%s\\%s", directory, find_data.cFileName);
            FindClose(hFind);
            return found_path;
        }
    } while (FindNextFileA(hFind, &find_data));

    FindClose(hFind);
    return NULL;
}

static char* SearchFileRecursive(const char* directory, const char* search_string) {
    if (!directory || !search_string || strlen(search_string) == 0) {
        return NULL;
    }

    char search_path[MAX_PATH];
    snprintf(search_path, sizeof(search_path), "%s\\*", directory);

    WIN32_FIND_DATAA find_data;
    HANDLE hFind = FindFirstFileA(search_path, &find_data);

    if (hFind == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) {
        return NULL;
    }

    static char found_path[MAX_PATH];

    do {
        
        if (strcmp(find_data.cFileName, ".") == 0 || strcmp(find_data.cFileName, "..") == 0) {
            continue;
        }

        char full_path[MAX_PATH];
        snprintf(full_path, sizeof(full_path), "%s\\%s", directory, find_data.cFileName);

        if (find_data.dwFileAttributes & FILE_ATTRIBUTE_DIRECTORY) {
            
            char* result = SearchFileRecursive(full_path, search_string);
            if (result != NULL) {
                FindClose(hFind);
                return result;
            }
        } else {
            
            if (StrStrIA(find_data.cFileName, search_string) != NULL) {
                snprintf(found_path, sizeof(found_path), "%s", full_path);
                FindClose(hFind);
                return found_path;
            }
        }
    } while (FindNextFileA(hFind, &find_data));

    FindClose(hFind);
    return NULL;
}

typedef struct {
    char name[512];
    char path[MAX_PATH];
    FILETIME last_write;
} FileItemWithTime;

static FileItemWithTime G_FILES_WITH_TIME[MAX_FILES];

static void GetFilesRecursiveWithTime(const char* dir_path, bool show_folder) {
    char search_path[MAX_PATH];
    snprintf(search_path, sizeof(search_path), "%s\\*", dir_path);

    WIN32_FIND_DATAA find_data;
    HANDLE hFind = FindFirstFileA(search_path, &find_data);

    if (hFind == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) return;

    do {
        if (strcmp(find_data.cFileName, ".") == 0 || strcmp(find_data.cFileName, "..") == 0) {
            continue;
        }

        char full_path[MAX_PATH];
        snprintf(full_path, sizeof(full_path), "%s\\%s", dir_path, find_data.cFileName);

        if (find_data.dwFileAttributes & FILE_ATTRIBUTE_DIRECTORY) {
            if (StrCmpIA(find_data.cFileName, "node_modules") == 0 ||
                StrCmpIA(find_data.cFileName, ".git") == 0 ||
                StrCmpIA(find_data.cFileName, "bin") == 0 ||
                StrCmpIA(find_data.cFileName, "obj") == 0 ||
                StrCmpIA(find_data.cFileName, "build") == 0) {
                continue;
            }
            GetFilesRecursiveWithTime(full_path, show_folder);
        } else {
            if (G_FILE_COUNT < MAX_FILES) {
                char clean_name[256];
                strncpy(clean_name, find_data.cFileName, sizeof(clean_name) - 1);
                PathRemoveExtensionA(clean_name);

                
                if (show_folder) {
                    char folder_name[MAX_PATH];
                    strncpy(folder_name, dir_path, sizeof(folder_name) - 1);
                    char* last_backslash = strrchr(folder_name, '\\');
                    if (last_backslash != NULL) {
                        snprintf(G_FILES_WITH_TIME[G_FILE_COUNT].name, sizeof(G_FILES_WITH_TIME[G_FILE_COUNT].name), 
                                 "%s/%s", last_backslash + 1, clean_name);
                    } else {
                        strncpy(G_FILES_WITH_TIME[G_FILE_COUNT].name, clean_name, 
                                sizeof(G_FILES_WITH_TIME[G_FILE_COUNT].name) - 1);
                    }
                } else {
                    strncpy(G_FILES_WITH_TIME[G_FILE_COUNT].name, clean_name, 
                            sizeof(G_FILES_WITH_TIME[G_FILE_COUNT].name) - 1);
                }

                strncpy(G_FILES_WITH_TIME[G_FILE_COUNT].path, full_path, 
                        sizeof(G_FILES_WITH_TIME[G_FILE_COUNT].path) - 1);
                
                
                G_FILES_WITH_TIME[G_FILE_COUNT].last_write = find_data.ftLastWriteTime;
                G_FILE_COUNT++;
            }
        }
    } while (FindNextFileA(hFind, &find_data) && G_FILE_COUNT < MAX_FILES);

    FindClose(hFind);
}
static int CompareFileByTime(const void* a, const void* b) {
    const FileItemWithTime* fa = (const FileItemWithTime*)a;
    const FileItemWithTime* fb = (const FileItemWithTime*)b;
    
    
    
    __int64 time_a = ((__int64)fa->last_write.dwHighDateTime << 32) + fa->last_write.dwLowDateTime;
    __int64 time_b = ((__int64)fb->last_write.dwHighDateTime << 32) + fb->last_write.dwLowDateTime;
    
    if (time_a > time_b) return -1;
    if (time_a < time_b) return 1;
    return 0;
}

static void SortFilesByTime(void) {
    qsort(G_FILES_WITH_TIME, G_FILE_COUNT, sizeof(FileItemWithTime), CompareFileByTime);
}

static void GetFilesRecursive(const char* dir_path, bool show_folder) {
    G_FILE_COUNT = 0;
    GetFilesRecursiveWithTime(dir_path, show_folder);
    SortFilesByTime();
    
    for (int i = 0; i < G_FILE_COUNT && i < MAX_FILES; i++) {
        strncpy(G_FILES[i].name, G_FILES_WITH_TIME[i].name, sizeof(G_FILES[i].name) - 1);
        G_FILES[i].name[sizeof(G_FILES[i].name) - 1] = '\0';
        strncpy(G_FILES[i].path, G_FILES_WITH_TIME[i].path, sizeof(G_FILES[i].path) - 1);
        G_FILES[i].path[sizeof(G_FILES[i].path) - 1] = '\0';
    }
}
static void GetFilesByExtension(const char* dir_path, const char* extensions[], int ext_count) {
    char search_path[MAX_PATH];
    snprintf(search_path, sizeof(search_path), "%s\\*", dir_path);

    WIN32_FIND_DATAA find_data;
    HANDLE hFind = FindFirstFileA(search_path, &find_data);

    if (hFind == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) return;

    do {
        if (strcmp(find_data.cFileName, ".") == 0 || strcmp(find_data.cFileName, "..") == 0) {
            continue;
        }

        char full_path[MAX_PATH];
        snprintf(full_path, sizeof(full_path), "%s\\%s", dir_path, find_data.cFileName);

        if (find_data.dwFileAttributes & FILE_ATTRIBUTE_DIRECTORY) {
            if (StrCmpIA(find_data.cFileName, "node_modules") == 0 ||
                StrCmpIA(find_data.cFileName, ".git") == 0 ||
                StrCmpIA(find_data.cFileName, "bin") == 0 ||
                StrCmpIA(find_data.cFileName, "obj") == 0 ||
                StrCmpIA(find_data.cFileName, "build") == 0) {
                continue;
            }
            GetFilesByExtension(full_path, extensions, ext_count);
        } else {
            
            char* ext = PathFindExtensionA(find_data.cFileName);
            for (int i = 0; i < ext_count; i++) {
                if (StrCmpIA(ext, extensions[i]) == 0) {
                    if (G_FILE_COUNT < MAX_FILES) {
                        strncpy(G_FILES[G_FILE_COUNT].name, find_data.cFileName, sizeof(G_FILES[G_FILE_COUNT].name) - 1);
                        strncpy(G_FILES[G_FILE_COUNT].path, full_path, sizeof(G_FILES[G_FILE_COUNT].path) - 1);
                        G_FILE_COUNT++;
                    }
                    break;
                }
            }
        }
    } while (FindNextFileA(hFind, &find_data) && G_FILE_COUNT < MAX_FILES);

    FindClose(hFind);
}

static FileItemWithTime* GetFileListWithTime(void) {
    return G_FILES_WITH_TIME;
}

static void ClearFileList(void) {
    G_FILE_COUNT = 0;
}


static FileItem* GetFileList(void) {
    return G_FILES;
}


static int GetFileCount(void) {
    return G_FILE_COUNT;
}
#define MAX_APPS 2000

typedef struct {
    char name[128];
    char path[MAX_PATH];
} AppItem;


AppItem G_APPS[MAX_APPS];
int G_APP_COUNT = 0;
static void scan_directory_recursive(const char* dir_path) {
    char search_path[MAX_PATH];
    snprintf(search_path, sizeof(search_path), "%s\\*", dir_path);

    WIN32_FIND_DATAA find_data;
    HANDLE hFind = FindFirstFileA(search_path, &find_data);

    if (hFind == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) return;

    do {
        if (strcmp(find_data.cFileName, ".") == 0 || strcmp(find_data.cFileName, "..") == 0) {
            continue;
        }

        char full_path[MAX_PATH];
        snprintf(full_path, sizeof(full_path), "%s\\%s", dir_path, find_data.cFileName);

        
        if (find_data.dwFileAttributes & FILE_ATTRIBUTE_DIRECTORY) {
            char first = tolower(find_data.cFileName[0]);
            
            if (first == 'n' || first == '.' || first == 'b' || first == 'o' || 
                first == 'd' || first == 'g' || first == 'y' || first == 'h') {
                
                bool skip = false;
                for (int i = 0; i <  sizeof(ListOfShitToIgnore) / sizeof(ListOfShitToIgnore[0]); i++) {
                    if (StrCmpIA(find_data.cFileName, ListOfShitToIgnore[i]) == 0) {
                        skip = true;
                        break;
                    }
                }
                if (skip) continue;
            }
            scan_directory_recursive(full_path);
        } else {
            char* ext = PathFindExtensionA(find_data.cFileName);
            if (StrCmpIA(ext, ".lnk") == 0 || StrCmpIA(ext, ".exe") == 0 || StrCmpIA(ext, ".url") == 0) {
                if (G_APP_COUNT < MAX_APPS) {
                    char clean_name[128];
                    strncpy(clean_name, find_data.cFileName, sizeof(clean_name) - 1);
                    clean_name[sizeof(clean_name) - 1] = '\0';
                    PathRemoveExtensionA(clean_name);

                    bool is_duplicate = false;
                    for (int i = 0; i < G_APP_COUNT; i++) {
                        if (StrCmpIA(G_APPS[i].name, clean_name) == 0) {
                            is_duplicate = true;
                            break;
                        }
                    }
                    if (is_duplicate) continue;

                    strncpy(G_APPS[G_APP_COUNT].name, clean_name, sizeof(G_APPS[G_APP_COUNT].name) - 1);
                    strncpy(G_APPS[G_APP_COUNT].path, full_path, sizeof(G_APPS[G_APP_COUNT].path) - 1);
                    G_APP_COUNT++;
                }
            }
        }
    } while (FindNextFileA(hFind, &find_data) && G_APP_COUNT < MAX_APPS);

    FindClose(hFind);
}

#endif


/* === FILE: focus_switcher.h === */
#ifndef FOCUS_SWITCHER_H
#define FOCUS_SWITCHER_H

#include <windows.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <shlwapi.h>
#include "config.h"

static void FocusWindow(HWND hwnd) {

    if (!hwnd || !IsWindow(hwnd)) return;
    
    HWND hForegroundWnd = GetForegroundWindow();
    DWORD foregroundThreadID = GetWindowThreadProcessId(hForegroundWnd, NULL);
    DWORD targetThreadID = GetWindowThreadProcessId(hwnd, NULL);
    DWORD currentThreadID = GetCurrentThreadId();

    if (foregroundThreadID != currentThreadID) {
        AttachThreadInput(currentThreadID, foregroundThreadID, TRUE);
    }
    if (targetThreadID != currentThreadID) {
        AttachThreadInput(currentThreadID, targetThreadID, TRUE);
    }

    if (IsIconic(hwnd)) {
        ShowWindow(hwnd, SW_RESTORE);
    } else {
        ShowWindow(hwnd, SW_SHOW);
    }

    SetForegroundWindow(hwnd);
    SetFocus(hwnd);
    SetActiveWindow(hwnd);
    BringWindowToTop(hwnd);

    if (foregroundThreadID != currentThreadID) {
        AttachThreadInput(currentThreadID, foregroundThreadID, FALSE);
    }
    if (targetThreadID != currentThreadID) {
        AttachThreadInput(currentThreadID, targetThreadID, FALSE);
    }
    
    printf("[FOCUS] Window focused!\n");
}
 
typedef struct {
    const char* search_exe;
    HWND result;
} FindWindowData;

static BOOL CALLBACK FindWindowByExeProc(HWND hwnd, LPARAM lParam) {
    FindWindowData* data = (FindWindowData*)lParam;
    DWORD processId = 0;
    GetWindowThreadProcessId(hwnd, &processId);
    
    if (processId != 0 && IsWindowVisible(hwnd)) {
        HANDLE hProcess = OpenProcess(PROCESS_QUERY_INFORMATION | PROCESS_VM_READ, FALSE, processId);
        if (hProcess) {
            char exe_name[MAX_PATH] = {0};
            DWORD size = MAX_PATH;
            if (GetModuleBaseNameA(hProcess, NULL, exe_name, size)) {
                printf("[DEBUG] Found exe: %s, searching for: %s\n", exe_name, data->search_exe);
                if (StrStrIA(exe_name, data->search_exe) != NULL) {
                    data->result = hwnd;
                    CloseHandle(hProcess);
                    return FALSE;
                }
            }
            CloseHandle(hProcess);
        }
    }
    return TRUE;
}

static bool FocusWindowByExe(const char* exe_name) {
    printf("[DEBUG] FocusWindowByExe called with: %s\n", exe_name);
    if (!exe_name || strlen(exe_name) == 0) return false;
    
    FindWindowData data;
    data.search_exe = exe_name;
    data.result = NULL;
    
    EnumWindows(FindWindowByExeProc, (LPARAM)&data);
    
    if (data.result) {
        FocusWindow(data.result);
        return true;
    } else {
        printf("[FOCUS] No window found with exe: %s\n", exe_name);
        return false;
    }
}

typedef struct {
    const char* search_title;
    HWND result;
} FindWindowSomething;

static BOOL CALLBACK FindWindowByPartialTitleProc(HWND hwnd, LPARAM lParam) {
    FindWindowSomething* data = (FindWindowSomething*)lParam;
    char window_title[256];
    UI_GetText(hwnd, window_title, sizeof(window_title));
    
    if (IsWindowVisible(hwnd) && StrStrIA(window_title, data->search_title) != NULL) {
        data->result = hwnd;
        return FALSE;
    }
    return TRUE;
}

static HWND FindWindowByPartialTitle(const char* title) {
    FindWindowSomething data;
    
    data.search_title = title;
    
    data.result = NULL;
    
    EnumWindows(FindWindowByPartialTitleProc, (LPARAM)&data);
    
    return data.result;
}




static HWND FindAnyWindow(const char* titles[], int count) {
    for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        printf("[FOCUS-DEBUG] Looking for: \"%s\"\n", titles[i]);
        
        
        HWND hwnd = FindWindowA(NULL, titles[i]);
        if (hwnd != NULL && IsWindow(hwnd) && IsWindowVisible(hwnd)) {
            printf("[FOCUS-DEBUG] FOUND (exact): \"%s\" (HWND: %p)\n", titles[i], (void*)hwnd);
            return hwnd;
        }
        
        
        hwnd = FindWindowByPartialTitle(titles[i]);
        if (hwnd != NULL && IsWindow(hwnd) && IsWindowVisible(hwnd)) {
            char window_title[256];
            UI_GetText(hwnd, window_title, sizeof(window_title));
            printf("[FOCUS-DEBUG] FOUND (partial): \"%s\" (HWND: %p)\n", window_title, (void*)hwnd);
            return hwnd;
        }
        
        printf("[FOCUS-DEBUG] NOT FOUND: \"%s\"\n", titles[i]);
    }
    printf("[FOCUS-DEBUG] No matching windows found.\n");
    return NULL;
}




static bool IsBlacklisted(const char* title, const char* blacklist[], int count) {
    for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        if (StrStrIA(title, blacklist[i]) != NULL) {
            return true;
        }
    }
    return false;
}




static bool HandleFocusSwitcher(int vk_code, int modifiers, const char* current_title) {
    for (int i = 0; i < FOCUS_SWITCHER_COUNT; i++) {
        const FocusSwitcherBinding* binding = &G_FOCUS_SWITCHERS[i];
        if (binding->vk_code == vk_code && binding->modifiers == modifiers) {
            if (IsBlacklisted(current_title, binding->blacklist, binding->blacklist_count)) {
                printf("[FOCUS] Blacklisted window: %s\n", current_title);
                return false;
            }
              if (strcmp(binding->window_titles[2],"Alacritty") == 0) {
                 if (FocusWindowByExe("alacritty.exe")) {
                   return true;
                 }else {
                    HWND target = FindAnyWindow(binding->window_titles, binding->window_count);
                    if (target) {
                        FocusWindow(target);
                    } else {
                        printf("[FOCUS] No matching window found.\n");
                    }
                 }
              }else { 
                  HWND target = FindAnyWindow(binding->window_titles, binding->window_count);
                  if (target) {
                      FocusWindow(target);
                      return true;
                  } else {
                      return false;
                      printf("[FOCUS] No matching window found.\n");
                  }
              }
              
        }
    }
    return false;
}

#endif


/* === FILE: google_search.h === */
#ifndef GOOGLE_SEARCH_H
#define GOOGLE_SEARCH_H

#include <windows.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <shellapi.h>
#include "types.h"
#include "helpers.h"
#include "config.h"

#define FILE_GOOGLE_ZOXIDE 5
#define GoogleZoxideFile G_FILE_ENTRIES[FILE_GOOGLE_ZOXIDE].mf

static char** g_cached_google_queries = NULL;
static int g_cached_google_count = 0;

static void RebuildGoogleCache(void) {
    if (g_cached_google_queries) {
        for (int i = 0; i < g_cached_google_count; i++) {
            free(g_cached_google_queries[i]);
        }
        free(g_cached_google_queries);
        g_cached_google_queries = NULL;
        g_cached_google_count = 0;
    }
    
    if (!GoogleZoxideFile || GoogleZoxideFile->fileSize == 0) {
        return;
    }
    
    size_t lineCount = 0;
    Line* lines = FileGetLines(GoogleZoxideFile, &lineCount);
    if (!lines) return;
    
    g_cached_google_queries = (char**)malloc(lineCount * sizeof(char*));
    g_cached_google_count = 0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < lineCount; i++) {
        size_t len = lines[i].length;
        while (len > 0 && lines[i].ptr[len-1] == '\r') len--;
        
        if (len > 0) {
            char* cmd = (char*)malloc(len + 1);
            memcpy(cmd, lines[i].ptr, len);
            cmd[len] = '\0';
            g_cached_google_queries[g_cached_google_count++] = cmd;
        }
    }
    
    free(lines);
}


static void google_refresh_callback(const char* filter, HWND hListBox) {
    if (!hListBox) return;
    
    if (!g_cached_google_queries) {
        RebuildGoogleCache();
    }
    
    PopulateListBox(hListBox, filter, (const char**)g_cached_google_queries, g_cached_google_count, NULL);
}


static void DoGoogleSearch(const char* query) {
    if (!query || strlen(query) == 0) return;
    
    char url[2048] = {0};
    char encoded_query[1024] = {0};
    
    // URL encode the query
    for (int i = 0; query[i] && i < sizeof(encoded_query) - 1; i++) {
        char c = query[i];
        if (c == ' ') {
            encoded_query[strlen(encoded_query)] = '+';
        } else if (c == '#') {
            strcat(encoded_query, "%23");
        } else if (c == '&') {
            strcat(encoded_query, "%26");
        } else if (c == '?') {
            strcat(encoded_query, "%3F");
        } else if (c == '%') {
            strcat(encoded_query, "%25");
        } else if (c == '+') {
            strcat(encoded_query, "%2B");
        } else {
            encoded_query[strlen(encoded_query)] = c;
        }
    }
    
    snprintf(url, sizeof(url), "https://www.google.com/search?q=%s", encoded_query);
    
    printf("[GOOGLE] Searching: %s\n", url);
    
    ShellExecuteWithVerb(url, "open", true);
    // ... rest of function ...
}

static void google_enter_callback(const char* selection) {
    int selected_index = LB_ERR;
    if (hListBox != NULL) {
        selected_index = UI_GetSelection(hListBox);
    }
    
    if (selected_index == LB_ERR) {
        char input[256] = {0};
        if (hEditControl != NULL) {
            UI_GetText(hEditControl, input, sizeof(input));
            if (strlen(input) > 0) {
                FileAppend(GoogleZoxideFile, input);
                FileAppend(GoogleZoxideFile, "\n");
                RebuildGoogleCache();
                printf("[GOOGLE] Saved query: %s\n", input);
                DoGoogleSearch(input);
            }
        }
    } else {
        DoGoogleSearch(selection);
    }
}

static void google_edit_callback(const char* selection) {
    char* NewgoogleQuery = EditTextPopup(selection,960,540,1000,300);
    
    if (NewgoogleQuery != NULL) {
        WriteToCacheIndex(g_SelectedIndex,NewgoogleQuery);
    }
    
    ReplaceLineInMappedFile(GoogleZoxideFile,g_SelectedIndex,NewgoogleQuery);
    
    RebuildGoogleCache();
    
    google_refresh_callback("",hListBox);
}

static void TriggerGoogleSearch() {
    if (CheckAndBlockIfPopupActive(hPopupWnd)) return;
    
    if (!g_cached_google_queries) {
        RebuildGoogleCache();
    }
    
    const char** list = (const char**)malloc(g_cached_google_count * sizeof(char*));
    if (!list) return;
    
    for (int i = 0; i < g_cached_google_count; i++) {
        list[i] = g_cached_google_queries[i];
    }
    
    ImportantPromptUser(list, "🔍 Google", g_cached_google_count, 920, 650, 32, 
               google_refresh_callback, 
               google_enter_callback, 
               google_edit_callback);
    
    free(list);
}

#endif


/* === FILE: helpers.h === */
#ifndef HELPERS_H
#define HELPERS_H

#include <stdbool.h>
#include <stdio.h>  
#include <string.h> 
#include <shlwapi.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include "types.h"
#include "config.h"
#include "common_ui.h"

typedef void (*HotkeyHandler)(void);

typedef struct {
    int vk_code;
    int modifiers;
    const char** blacklist;
    int blacklist_count;
    HotkeyHandler handler;
    const char* name;
    bool condition;
} HotkeyBinding;

static inline bool ProcessHotkey(const HotkeyBinding* bindings, int bindings_count,
                                  int pressed_vk, int modifiers,
                                  const char* current_title) {
    for (int i = 0; i < bindings_count; i++) {
        const HotkeyBinding* b = &bindings[i];
        
        if (b->vk_code != pressed_vk) continue;
        if (b->modifiers != modifiers) continue;
        
        if (!b->condition) {
            continue;
        }
        
        if (b->blacklist && b->blacklist_count > 0) {
            bool blacklisted = false;
            for (int j = 0; j < b->blacklist_count; j++) {
                if (StrStrIA(current_title, b->blacklist[j]) != NULL) {
                    blacklisted = true;
                    break;
                }
            }
            if (blacklisted) return false;
        }
        
        if (IsPopupActive(hPopupWnd)) return false;
        
        if (b->handler) {
            b->handler();
            return true;
        }
    }
    return false;
}


typedef struct {
    const char* prompt_title;
    const char* icon;
    bool open_neovide;
    bool open_yazi;
    bool open_explorer;
} ZoxideOptions;

static inline char* QueryZoxidePath(const char* query) {
    DWORD start_time = GetTickCount();

    static char path[1024];
    path[0] = '\0';

    char clean_query[512];
    strncpy(clean_query, query, sizeof(clean_query) - 1);
    clean_query[sizeof(clean_query) - 1] = '\0';
    clean_query[strcspn(clean_query, "\r\n")] = '\0';


    char command[512];
    snprintf(command, sizeof(command), "zoxide query %s", clean_query);

    SECURITY_ATTRIBUTES sa = { sizeof(SECURITY_ATTRIBUTES), NULL, TRUE };
    HANDLE hReadPipe, hWritePipe;

    if (!CreatePipe(&hReadPipe, &hWritePipe, &sa, 0)) {
        return NULL;
    }

    SetHandleInformation(hReadPipe, HANDLE_FLAG_INHERIT, 0);

    STARTUPINFOA si = {0};
    si.cb = sizeof(STARTUPINFOA);
    si.hStdOutput = hWritePipe;
    si.hStdError = hWritePipe;
    si.dwFlags |= STARTF_USESTDHANDLES;

    PROCESS_INFORMATION pi = {0};

    DWORD proc_start = GetTickCount();
    if (CreateProcessA(NULL, command, NULL, NULL, TRUE, CREATE_NO_WINDOW, NULL, NULL, &si, &pi)) {
        CloseHandle(hWritePipe);

        DWORD wait_res = WaitForSingleObject(pi.hProcess, 5000); 

        if (wait_res == WAIT_TIMEOUT) {
            TerminateProcess(pi.hProcess, 0);
        }

        DWORD bytesRead = 0;
        if (ReadFile(hReadPipe, path, sizeof(path) - 1, &bytesRead, NULL)) {
            path[bytesRead] = '\0';
            path[strcspn(path, "\r\n")] = '\0';
        }

        CloseHandle(pi.hProcess);
        CloseHandle(pi.hThread);
    } else {
        CloseHandle(hWritePipe);
    }

    CloseHandle(hReadPipe);
    if (strstr(path, "no match found")) {
        char buffer[256];
        snprintf(buffer,sizeof(buffer),"Query {%s} Doesnt Have A Zoxide Path",query);
        DisplayMessageText = RGB(255,0,0);
        MSG_BOTTOM(buffer,140,1);
        return NULL;
    }
    return (strlen(path) > 0) ? path : NULL;
}

static inline bool ShellExecuteWithVerb(const char* path, const char* verb, bool async) {
    SHELLEXECUTEINFOA sei = {0};
    sei.cbSize = sizeof(SHELLEXECUTEINFOA);
    sei.fMask = (async ? SEE_MASK_FLAG_NO_UI | SEE_MASK_ASYNCOK : SEE_MASK_FLAG_NO_UI);
    sei.lpVerb = verb ? verb : "open";
    sei.lpFile = path;
    sei.nShow = SW_SHOWNORMAL;
    return ShellExecuteExA(&sei);
}

static inline bool LaunchApp(const char* path) {
    return ShellExecuteWithVerb(path, "open", true);
}

static inline bool LaunchAppAsAdmin(const char* path) {
    return ShellExecuteWithVerb(path, "runas", true);
}
static inline bool IsCtrlPressed(void) {
    return (GetKeyState(VK_CONTROL) & 0x8000) != 0;
}





typedef struct {
    size_t index;
    char original;
} NewlineInfo;

static inline int FileInit(const char *filePath, MappedFile *mf, size_t initialPadding) {

    mf->hFile = CreateFileA(filePath, GENERIC_READ | GENERIC_WRITE, 0, NULL, OPEN_ALWAYS, FILE_ATTRIBUTE_NORMAL, NULL);
    if (mf->hFile == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) {
        return 0;
    }

    LARGE_INTEGER fs;
    GetFileSizeEx(mf->hFile, &fs);
    mf->fileSize = (size_t)fs.QuadPart;
    
    
    
    size_t mapSize = (mf->fileSize > 0) ? mf->fileSize : 1;
    mf->capacity = mapSize;

    LARGE_INTEGER mapSizeLI;
    mapSizeLI.QuadPart = mapSize;

    mf->hMapping = CreateFileMappingA(mf->hFile, NULL, PAGE_READWRITE, mapSizeLI.HighPart, mapSizeLI.LowPart, NULL);
    if (!mf->hMapping) {
        CloseHandle(mf->hFile);
        return 0;
    }

    mf->pBuffer = (char *)MapViewOfFile(mf->hMapping, FILE_MAP_WRITE, 0, 0, 0);
    if (!mf->pBuffer) {
        CloseHandle(mf->hMapping);
        CloseHandle(mf->hFile);
        return 0;
    }

    
    if (mf->fileSize == 0) {
        mf->pBuffer[0] = '\0';
    }

    return 1;
}

static inline int FileAppend(MappedFile *mf, const char *text) {
    if (!mf || !text) return 0;
    
    size_t len = strlen(text);
    if (len == 0) return 0;

    
    if (mf->fileSize + len + 1 > mf->capacity) {
        
        if (mf->pBuffer) {
            UnmapViewOfFile(mf->pBuffer);
            mf->pBuffer = NULL;
        }
        if (mf->hMapping) {
            CloseHandle(mf->hMapping);
            mf->hMapping = NULL;
        }

        
        size_t newCapacity = mf->fileSize + len + 1;
        LARGE_INTEGER mapSizeLI;
        mapSizeLI.QuadPart = newCapacity;

        
        LARGE_INTEGER newFileSize;
        newFileSize.QuadPart = newCapacity;
        SetFilePointerEx(mf->hFile, newFileSize, NULL, FILE_BEGIN);
        SetEndOfFile(mf->hFile);

        
        mf->hMapping = CreateFileMappingA(mf->hFile, NULL, PAGE_READWRITE, mapSizeLI.HighPart, mapSizeLI.LowPart, NULL);
        if (!mf->hMapping) {
            return 0;
        }

        mf->pBuffer = (char *)MapViewOfFile(mf->hMapping, FILE_MAP_WRITE, 0, 0, 0);
        if (!mf->pBuffer) {
            CloseHandle(mf->hMapping);
            mf->hMapping = NULL;
            return 0;
        }
        mf->capacity = newCapacity;
    }

    
    memcpy(mf->pBuffer + mf->fileSize, text, len);
    mf->fileSize += len;
    mf->pBuffer[mf->fileSize] = '\0';

    
    LARGE_INTEGER fs;
    fs.QuadPart = mf->fileSize;
    SetFilePointerEx(mf->hFile, fs, NULL, FILE_BEGIN);
    SetEndOfFile(mf->hFile);

    return 1;
}

static inline void FileClose(MappedFile *mf) {
    if (!mf) return;
    
    if (mf->pBuffer) {
        FlushViewOfFile(mf->pBuffer, mf->fileSize);
        UnmapViewOfFile(mf->pBuffer);
        mf->pBuffer = NULL;
    }
    
    if (mf->hMapping) {
        CloseHandle(mf->hMapping);
        mf->hMapping = NULL;
    }
    
    if (mf->hFile != INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) {
        
        LARGE_INTEGER fs;
        fs.QuadPart = mf->fileSize;
        SetFilePointerEx(mf->hFile, fs, NULL, FILE_BEGIN);
        SetEndOfFile(mf->hFile);
        CloseHandle(mf->hFile);
        mf->hFile = INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE;
    }
}
static inline Line* FileGetLines(MappedFile *mf, size_t *outLineCount) {
    DWORD start = GetTickCount();
    size_t maxLines = 1000;
    size_t count = 0;
    Line *list = (Line *)malloc(maxLines * sizeof(Line));
    if (list == NULL) return NULL;

    char *lineStart = mf->pBuffer;
    size_t totalBytes = mf->fileSize;

    for (size_t i = 0; i < totalBytes; i++) {
        if (mf->pBuffer[i] == '\n' || i == totalBytes - 1) {
            if (count >= maxLines) {
                maxLines *= 2;
                list = (Line *)realloc(list, maxLines * sizeof(Line));
            }
            size_t currentLineLen = &mf->pBuffer[i] - lineStart;
            if (i == totalBytes - 1) currentLineLen++; 
            list[count].ptr = lineStart;
            list[count].length = currentLineLen;
            count++;
            lineStart = &mf->pBuffer[i + 1];
        }
    }
    *outLineCount = count;
    return list;
}

static inline char** FileGetStrings(MappedFile *mf, size_t *outLineCount, NewlineInfo **outNewlines, size_t *outNewlineCount) {
    DWORD start = GetTickCount();
    size_t maxLines = 1000;
    size_t count = 0;
    
    char **stringList = (char **)malloc(maxLines * sizeof(char*));
    if (stringList == NULL) return NULL;

    size_t maxNewlines = 2000;
    size_t newlineCount = 0;
    NewlineInfo *newlines = (NewlineInfo *)malloc(maxNewlines * sizeof(NewlineInfo));
    if (newlines == NULL) {
        free(stringList);
        return NULL;
    }

    stringList[count++] = mf->pBuffer;
    size_t totalBytes = mf->fileSize;

    for (size_t i = 0; i < totalBytes; i++) {
        if (mf->pBuffer[i] == '\r') {
            char orig = mf->pBuffer[i];
            mf->pBuffer[i] = '\0';
            
            if (newlineCount >= maxNewlines) {
                maxNewlines *= 2;
                newlines = (NewlineInfo *)realloc(newlines, maxNewlines * sizeof(NewlineInfo));
            }
            newlines[newlineCount++] = (NewlineInfo){ .index = i, .original = orig };
        }
        else if (mf->pBuffer[i] == '\n') {
            char orig = mf->pBuffer[i];
            mf->pBuffer[i] = '\0';
            
            if (newlineCount >= maxNewlines) {
                maxNewlines *= 2;
                newlines = (NewlineInfo *)realloc(newlines, maxNewlines * sizeof(NewlineInfo));
            }
            newlines[newlineCount++] = (NewlineInfo){ .index = i, .original = orig };

            if (i + 1 < totalBytes) {
                if (count >= maxLines) {
                    maxLines *= 2;
                    stringList = (char **)realloc(stringList, maxLines * sizeof(char*));
                }
                stringList[count++] = &mf->pBuffer[i + 1];
            }
        }
    }
    
    *outLineCount = count;
    *outNewlines = newlines; 
    *outNewlineCount = newlineCount;
    return stringList;
}

void CleanUpFile(MappedFile *mf, NewlineInfo *newlines, size_t newlineCount) {
    if (!newlines) return;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < newlineCount; i++) {
        mf->pBuffer[newlines[i].index] = newlines[i].original;
    }
    free(newlines);
}

static inline void SanitizeFile(const char *filePath) {
    HANDLE hFile = CreateFileA(filePath, GENERIC_READ | GENERIC_WRITE, 0, NULL, OPEN_ALWAYS, FILE_ATTRIBUTE_NORMAL, NULL);
    if (hFile == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) return;

    LARGE_INTEGER fs;
    GetFileSizeEx(hFile, &fs);
    DWORD fileSize = (DWORD)fs.QuadPart;
    if (fileSize == 0) { CloseHandle(hFile); return; }

    char *buf = (char *)malloc(fileSize);
    if (!buf) { CloseHandle(hFile); return; }

    DWORD bytesRead = 0;
    ReadFile(hFile, buf, fileSize, &bytesRead, NULL);

    DWORD cleanCount = 0;
    for (DWORD i = 0; i < bytesRead; i++) {
        if (buf[i] != '\0') {
            buf[cleanCount++] = buf[i];
        }
    }

    if (cleanCount < bytesRead) {
        SetFilePointer(hFile, 0, NULL, FILE_BEGIN);
        WriteFile(hFile, buf, cleanCount, &bytesRead, NULL);
        SetEndOfFile(hFile);
        printf("[SANITIZE] Cleaned %lu null bytes from %s\n", bytesRead - cleanCount, filePath);
    }

    free(buf);
    CloseHandle(hFile);
}

typedef struct {
    char* flag;
    int index;
} FoundFlag;

// Parse time string like "5m", "10h", "20s" into seconds
static inline int ParseTimeToSeconds(const char* input) {
    if (!input || strlen(input) == 0) return 0;
    
    int total = 0;
    char buffer[64];
    strncpy(buffer, input, sizeof(buffer) - 1);
    buffer[sizeof(buffer) - 1] = '\0';
    
    char* token = strtok(buffer, " ");
    while (token != NULL) {
        int value = 0;
        char unit = 's';
        int len = strlen(token);
        
        if (len > 1) {
            char last = tolower(token[len - 1]);
            if (last == 's' || last == 'm' || last == 'h' || last == 'd') {
                token[len - 1] = '\0';
                value = atoi(token);
                unit = last;
            } else {
                value = atoi(token);
            }
        } else {
            value = atoi(token);
        }
        
        switch (unit) {
            case 's': total += value; break;
            case 'm': total += value * 60; break;
            case 'h': total += value * 3600; break;
            case 'd': total += value * 86400; break;
            default: total += value; break;
        }
        
        token = strtok(NULL, " ");
    }
    
    return total;
}
typedef void (*ActionFn)(const char* parameter);

typedef bool (*ConditionFn)(const char* parameter);

typedef void (*TimerFn)(const char* parameter);

typedef struct { char* str; ActionFn    func; } ActionDef;
typedef struct { char* str; ConditionFn func; } ConditionDef;
typedef struct { char* str; TimerFn     func; } TimerDef;

typedef struct {
    char* name;
    ConditionFn func;
} ConditionParamDef;

static inline int SearchFlagsInText(const char* text, const void* flag_array, int flag_count, size_t flag_size, FoundFlag* matches, int max_matches) {
    if (!text || !flag_array || !matches) return 0;
    
    int match_count = 0;
    char* text_copy = _strdup(text);
    if (!text_copy) return 0;
    
    // Convert to lowercase for case-insensitive search
    for (int i = 0; text_copy[i]; i++) {
        text_copy[i] = tolower(text_copy[i]);
    }
    
    for (int i = 0; i < flag_count && match_count < max_matches; i++) {
        const char* flag_str = NULL;
        
        // Extract flag string based on flag_size
        if (flag_size == sizeof(char*)) {
            flag_str = ((char**)flag_array)[i];
        } else if (flag_size == sizeof(ActionDef)) {
            flag_str = ((ActionDef*)flag_array)[i].str;
        } else if (flag_size == sizeof(ConditionDef)) {
            flag_str = ((ConditionDef*)flag_array)[i].str;
        } else if (flag_size == sizeof(TimerDef)) {
            flag_str = ((TimerDef*)flag_array)[i].str;
        } else if (flag_size == sizeof(GenericFlagEntry)) {
            flag_str = ((const GenericFlagEntry*)flag_array)[i].flag;
        }
        
        if (!flag_str) continue;
        
        char* found = strstr(text_copy, flag_str);
        if (found) {
            // === EXACT MATCH CHECK ===
            // Make sure this is a whole word, not part of another flag
            
            // Check if character before match is start of string or space
            int before_ok = (found == text_copy || *(found - 1) == ' ');
            
            // Check if character after match is end of string, space, or not a letter
            int after_ok = 1;
            char* after = found + strlen(flag_str);
            if (*after != '\0' && *after != ' ' && *after != '\t') {
                after_ok = 0;
            }
            
            if (before_ok && after_ok) {
                matches[match_count].flag = (char*)flag_str;
                matches[match_count].index = found - text_copy;
                match_count++;
            }
        }
    }
    
    free(text_copy);
    return match_count;
}

static inline char* GetFlagParameter(const char* text, int flag_index) {
    if (!text || flag_index < 0) return NULL;
    
    static char param[256];
    param[0] = '\0';
    
    // Skip past the flag
    int pos = flag_index;
    while (text[pos] && text[pos] != ' ') pos++;
    while (text[pos] && text[pos] == ' ') pos++;
    
    // Find next flag (must be at start of word, with space before or at start)
    int start = pos;
    while (text[pos]) {
        // Check if this is the start of a flag
        if (text[pos] == '-' && (pos == 0 || text[pos-1] == ' ')) {
            break;
        }
        pos++;
    }
    
    int len = pos - start;
    if (len > 0) {
        strncpy(param, text + start, len);
        param[len] = '\0';
        
        // Trim trailing spaces
        char* end = param + strlen(param) - 1;
        while (end > param && *end == ' ') end--;
        *(end + 1) = '\0';
        
        return param;
    }
    
    return NULL;
}

static inline char* GetCleanText(const char* text) {
    if (!text) return NULL;
    
    static char clean[256];
    clean[0] = '\0';
    
    const char* flags[] = {"-f", "-cg", "-trw", "-tr", "-t"};
    int flag_count = sizeof(flags) / sizeof(flags[0]);
    
    char* text_copy = _strdup(text);
    if (!text_copy) return NULL;
    
    char* result = text_copy;
    char* write_ptr = text_copy;
    
    while (*result) {
        int skip = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < flag_count; i++) {
            if (strncmp(result, flags[i], strlen(flags[i])) == 0) {
                skip = 1;
                result += strlen(flags[i]);
                while (*result && *result == ' ') result++;
                while (*result && *result != ' ') result++;
                while (*result && *result == ' ') result++;
                break;
            }
        }
        
        if (!skip) {
            *write_ptr = *result;
            write_ptr++;
            result++;
        }
    }
    
    *write_ptr = '\0';
    
    char* start = text_copy;
    while (*start == ' ') start++;
    char* end = text_copy + strlen(text_copy) - 1;
    while (end > start && *end == ' ') end--;
    *(end + 1) = '\0';
    
    strncpy(clean, start, sizeof(clean) - 1);
    clean[sizeof(clean) - 1] = '\0';
    
    free(text_copy);
    return clean;
}

static bool IsWindowBlacklisted(const char* window_title, const char* blacklist[], int count) {
    for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        if (StrStrIA(window_title, blacklist[i]) != NULL) {
            return true;
        }
    }
    return false;
}

static ULONGLONG g_timer_start_ms = 0;

void StartUniversalTimer() {
    g_timer_start_ms = GetTickCount64();
}

void ResetUniversalTimer() {
    g_timer_start_ms = GetTickCount64();
}

void ModifyUniversalTimer(double seconds_to_add) {
    LONGLONG ms_offset = (LONGLONG)(seconds_to_add * 1000.0);
    g_timer_start_ms -= ms_offset; 
    printf("[TIMER] Modified baseline by %.2f seconds.\n", seconds_to_add);
}

double GetUniversalTimeSeconds() {
    ULONGLONG current_ms = GetTickCount64();
    
    if (current_ms < g_timer_start_ms) {
        return 0.0;
    }
    
    ULONGLONG elapsed_ms = current_ms - g_timer_start_ms;
    return (double)elapsed_ms / 1000.0;
}

int CopyToClipboard(const char* text) {
    if (text == NULL) return 0;

    size_t len = strlen(text);
    if (len == 0) return 0;

    char* clean_text = (char*)malloc(len + 1);
    if (clean_text == NULL) return 0;

    size_t write_idx = 0;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < len; i++) {
        if (text[i] != '\r') {
            clean_text[write_idx++] = text[i];
        }
    }
    clean_text[write_idx] = '\0';

    len = write_idx;
    if (len == 0) {
        free(clean_text);
        return 0;
    }

    HGLOBAL hMem = GlobalAlloc(GMEM_MOVEABLE, len + 1);
    if (hMem == NULL) {
        free(clean_text);
        return 0;
    }

    char* pMem = (char*)GlobalLock(hMem);
    if (pMem == NULL) {
        GlobalFree(hMem);
        free(clean_text);
        return 0;
    }
    
    memcpy(pMem, clean_text, len + 1); 
    GlobalUnlock(hMem);
    free(clean_text);

    if (!OpenClipboard(NULL)) {
        GlobalFree(hMem);
        return 0;
    }

    if (!EmptyClipboard()) {
        GlobalFree(hMem);
        CloseClipboard();
        return 0;
    }

    if (SetClipboardData(CF_TEXT, hMem) == NULL) {
        GlobalFree(hMem);
        CloseClipboard();
        return 0;
    }
    printf("%s",text);
    char buffer[256];
    
    snprintf(buffer,sizeof(buffer),"Copied %s",text);
    
    if (hPopupWnd != NULL) {
      MSG_TOP(buffer,140,1);
    }else {
      MSG_MIDDLE_BOTTOM(buffer,70,1);
    }
    
    CloseClipboard();
    
    return 1;
}
static unsigned int hash(const char* key, int size) {
    unsigned int h = 0;
    while (*key) {
        h = (h * 31) ^ (unsigned char)*key;
        key++;
    }
    return h % size;
}

static inline HashMap* HashMap_Create(int initial_size) {
    HashMap* map = malloc(sizeof(HashMap));
    if (!map) return NULL;
    
    map->size = initial_size ? initial_size : 32;
    map->count = 0;
    map->buckets = calloc(map->size, sizeof(HashMapEntry*));
    
    return map;
}

static inline void HashMap_Destroy(HashMap* map) {
    if (!map) return;
    
    for (int i = 0; i < map->size; i++) {
        HashMapEntry* entry = map->buckets[i];
        while (entry) {
            HashMapEntry* next = entry->next;
            free(entry->key);
            free(entry);
            entry = next;
        }
    }
    
    free(map->buckets);
    free(map);
}

static inline void HashMap_Put(HashMap* map, const char* key, void* value) {
    if (!map || !key) return;
    
    unsigned int index = hash(key, map->size);
    HashMapEntry* entry = map->buckets[index];
    
    // Check if key already exists
    while (entry) {
        if (strcmp(entry->key, key) == 0) {
            entry->value = value;
            return;
        }
        entry = entry->next;
    }
    
    // Add new entry
    HashMapEntry* new_entry = malloc(sizeof(HashMapEntry));
    new_entry->key = _strdup(key);
    new_entry->value = value;
    new_entry->next = map->buckets[index];
    map->buckets[index] = new_entry;
    map->count++;
}

static inline void* HashMap_Get(HashMap* map, const char* key) {
    if (!map || !key) return NULL;
    
    unsigned int index = hash(key, map->size);
    HashMapEntry* entry = map->buckets[index];
    
    while (entry) {
        if (strcmp(entry->key, key) == 0) {
            return entry->value;
        }
        entry = entry->next;
    }
    
    return NULL;
}

static inline bool HashMap_Contains(HashMap* map, const char* key) {
    return HashMap_Get(map, key) != NULL;
}

static inline void* HashMap_Remove(HashMap* map, const char* key) {
    if (!map || !key) return NULL;
    
    unsigned int index = hash(key, map->size);
    HashMapEntry* entry = map->buckets[index];
    HashMapEntry* prev = NULL;
    
    while (entry) {
        if (strcmp(entry->key, key) == 0) {
            if (prev) {
                prev->next = entry->next;
            } else {
                map->buckets[index] = entry->next;
            }
            
            void* value = entry->value;
            free(entry->key);
            free(entry);
            map->count--;
            return value;
        }
        prev = entry;
        entry = entry->next;
    }
    
    return NULL;
}

static inline char** HashMap_GetKeys(HashMap* map, int* count) {
    if (!map) return NULL;
    
    char** keys = malloc(map->count * sizeof(char*));
    int idx = 0;
    
    for (int i = 0; i < map->size; i++) {
        HashMapEntry* entry = map->buckets[i];
        while (entry) {
            keys[idx++] = entry->key;
            entry = entry->next;
        }
    }
    
    *count = map->count;
    return keys;
}
static inline char** StringList_Append(char** list, int* count, const char* str) {
    if (!str) return list;
    
    // Reallocate list to hold one more pointer
    list = realloc(list, (*count + 2) * sizeof(char*));
    if (!list) return NULL;
    
    // Copy the string
    list[*count] = _strdup(str);
    (*count)++;
    list[*count] = NULL;  // Null-terminate
    
    return list;
}

static inline void StringList_Free(char** list, int count) {
    if (!list) return;
    for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        free(list[i]);
    }
    free(list);
}

static inline int HashMap_Size(HashMap* map) {
    return map ? map->count : 0;
}

static inline void SaveFeatureHistory(char* string) {
    char** HistoryList = NULL;
    int count = 0;
    
    if (HashMap_Contains(feature_history, g_current_feature)) {
        HistoryList = (char**)HashMap_Get(feature_history, g_current_feature);
        while (HistoryList && HistoryList[count]) count++;
    }
    
    HistoryList = StringList_Append(HistoryList, &count, string);
    
    HashMap_Put(feature_history, g_current_feature, HistoryList);
}

static inline struct feature_data* GetFeatureData(const char* name) {
    if (!feature_data) {
        feature_data = HashMap_Create(32);
    }
    
    struct feature_data* data = (struct feature_data*)HashMap_Get(feature_data, name);
    
    if (!data) {
        data = malloc(sizeof(struct feature_data));
        if (data) {
            memset(data, 0, sizeof(struct feature_data));
            data->name = _strdup(name);
            HashMap_Put(feature_data, name, data);
            
            struct feature_data* verify = (struct feature_data*)HashMap_Get(feature_data, name);
        }
    }
    return data;
}

static inline void SaveFeatureData(char* string) {
    if (!feature_data) return;
    
    struct feature_data* data = GetFeatureData(g_current_feature);
}

static inline void RegisterFeature(const char* name, const char** items, int item_count,
                                   RefreshCallback refresh_cb,
                                   EnterCallback enter_cb,
                                   CtrlEnterCallback ctrl_enter_cb,
                                   TabCallback tab_cb,
                                   RenderCallback render_cb,
                                   EditCallback edit_cb,
                                   FilterCallback filter_cb,
                                   bool (*filter_match)(const char*, const char*)) {
    struct feature_data* data = GetFeatureData(name);
    
    if (data->sort_options_count > 0) {
        data->current_sort_index = 0; 
    } else {
        data->current_sort_index = -1;
    }
    
    data->items = items;
    data->item_count = item_count;
    data->refresh_callback = refresh_cb;
    data->enter_callback = enter_cb;
    data->ctrl_enter_callback = ctrl_enter_cb;
    data->tab_callback = tab_cb;
    data->render_callback = render_cb;
    data->edit_callback = edit_cb;
    data->filter_callback = filter_cb;
    data->filter_match = filter_match;
}

static inline void GetFeatureCallbacks(const char* name,
                                       RefreshCallback* refresh,
                                       EnterCallback* enter,
                                       CtrlEnterCallback* ctrl_enter,
                                       TabCallback* tab,
                                       RenderCallback* render,
                                       EditCallback* edit,
                                       FilterCallback* filter) {
    struct feature_data* data = GetFeatureData(name);
    if (data) {
        if (refresh) *refresh = data->refresh_callback;
        if (enter) *enter = data->enter_callback;
        if (ctrl_enter) *ctrl_enter = data->ctrl_enter_callback;
        if (tab) *tab = data->tab_callback;
        if (render) *render = data->render_callback;
        if (edit) *edit = data->edit_callback;
        if (filter) *filter = data->filter_callback;
    }
}
static int ReplaceLineInFile(const char* filepath, int line_index, const char* new_text) {
    if (!filepath || !new_text || line_index < 0) {
        return 0;
    }
    
    FILE* f = fopen(filepath, "r");
    if (!f) {
        return 0;
    }
    
    char lines[100][512];
    int count = 0;
    while (fgets(lines[count], sizeof(lines[count]), f) && count < 100) {
        lines[count][strcspn(lines[count], "\r\n")] = '\0';
        count++;
    }
    fclose(f);
    
    if (line_index >= count) {
        return 0;
    }
    
    
    strncpy(lines[line_index], new_text, sizeof(lines[line_index]) - 1);
    lines[line_index][sizeof(lines[line_index]) - 1] = '\0';
    
    f = fopen(filepath, "w");
    if (!f) {
        return 0;
    }
    
    for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        fprintf(f, "%s\n", lines[i]);
    }
    fclose(f);
    
    return 1;
}
static int ReplaceLineInMappedFile(MappedFile* mf, int line_index, const char* new_text) {
    if (!mf || !mf->pBuffer || !new_text || line_index < 0) return 0;
    
    // Get all lines
    size_t lineCount = 0;
    Line* lines = FileGetLines(mf, &lineCount);
    if (!lines) return 0;
    
    // Check if index is valid
    if (line_index >= lineCount) {
        free(lines);
        return 0;
    }
    
    // Calculate the new file size
    size_t new_size = 0;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < lineCount; i++) {
        if (i == line_index) {
            // Use new text for this line
            new_size += strlen(new_text) + 1; // +1 for newline
        } else {
            new_size += lines[i].length + 1; // +1 for newline
        }
    }
    
    // Allocate new buffer
    char* new_buffer = (char*)malloc(new_size + 1);
    if (!new_buffer) {
        free(lines);
        return 0;
    }
    
    // Build new file content
    char* ptr = new_buffer;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < lineCount; i++) {
        if (i == line_index) {
            // Write new text
            int len = strlen(new_text);
            memcpy(ptr, new_text, len);
            ptr += len;
        } else {
            // Write existing line
            memcpy(ptr, lines[i].ptr, lines[i].length);
            ptr += lines[i].length;
        }
        // Add newline
        *ptr = '\n';
        ptr++;
    }
    *ptr = '\0';
    
    // Resize the mapped file
    HANDLE hFile = mf->hFile;
    
    // Unmap and close old mapping
    if (mf->pBuffer) {
        FlushViewOfFile(mf->pBuffer, mf->fileSize);
        UnmapViewOfFile(mf->pBuffer);
        mf->pBuffer = NULL;
    }
    if (mf->hMapping) {
        CloseHandle(mf->hMapping);
        mf->hMapping = NULL;
    }
    
    // Set new file size
    LARGE_INTEGER newFileSize;
    newFileSize.QuadPart = new_size;
    SetFilePointerEx(hFile, newFileSize, NULL, FILE_BEGIN);
    SetEndOfFile(hFile);
    
    // Create new mapping
    LARGE_INTEGER mapSizeLI;
    mapSizeLI.QuadPart = new_size;
    mf->hMapping = CreateFileMappingA(hFile, NULL, PAGE_READWRITE, 
                                       mapSizeLI.HighPart, mapSizeLI.LowPart, NULL);
    if (!mf->hMapping) {
        free(lines);
        free(new_buffer);
        return 0;
    }
    
    mf->pBuffer = (char*)MapViewOfFile(mf->hMapping, FILE_MAP_WRITE, 0, 0, 0);
    if (!mf->pBuffer) {
        CloseHandle(mf->hMapping);
        mf->hMapping = NULL;
        free(lines);
        free(new_buffer);
        return 0;
    }
    
    // Copy new content to mapped file
    memcpy(mf->pBuffer, new_buffer, new_size);
    mf->fileSize = new_size;
    
    free(lines);
    free(new_buffer);
    return 1;
}
static void WriteToCacheIndex(int index, const char* text) {
    if (index < 0 || index >= MAX_ITEMS || !text) return;
    
    strncpy(g_populated_cache[index], text, sizeof(g_populated_cache[index]) - 1);
    g_populated_cache[index][sizeof(g_populated_cache[index]) - 1] = '\0';
}

int ReadMdFile(char lines[100][512], const char* path) {
    FILE* f = fopen(path, "r");
    if (!f) return 0;
    int count = 0;
    char buffer[512];
    while (fgets(buffer, sizeof(buffer), f) && count < 100) {
        buffer[strcspn(buffer, "\r\n")] = '\0';
        if (strlen(buffer) > 0) {
            strncpy(lines[count], buffer, 511);
            lines[count][511] = '\0';
            count++;
        }
    }
    fclose(f);
    return count;
}
void StripCheckboxes(char* str) {
    char* read_ptr = str;
    char* write_ptr = str;
    bool is_first = true;

    while (*read_ptr != '\0') {
        if (strncmp(read_ptr, "- [ ] ", 6) == 0) {
            if (!is_first) {
                *write_ptr = ','; 
                write_ptr++;
            }
            is_first = false; 
            read_ptr += 6;
        } else {
            *write_ptr = *read_ptr;
            write_ptr++;
            read_ptr++;
        }
    }
    *write_ptr = '\0';
}

void WriteMdFile(char lines[100][512], int count, const char* path) {
    FILE* f = fopen(path, "w");
    if (!f) return;
    for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        fprintf(f, "%s\n", lines[i]);
    }
    fclose(f);
}
HashMap* history_indices = NULL;

void GetFromHistory() {
    char** HistoryList = (char**)HashMap_Get(feature_history, g_current_feature);
    if (HistoryList) {
        int* idx_ptr = (int*)HashMap_Get(history_indices, g_current_feature);
        int idx = idx_ptr ? *idx_ptr : 0;
        
        int count = 0;
        while (HistoryList[count]) count++;
        
        if (count > 0) {
            idx = (idx - 1 + count) % count;
            
            UI_SetText(hEditControl, HistoryList[idx]);
            UI_SetCursorPosition(hEditControl,strlen(HistoryList[idx]));

            if (!IsNormalMode()) {
                if (g_refresh_callback != NULL) {
                    g_refresh_callback(HistoryList[idx], hListBox);
                }
            }
            if (!idx_ptr) {
                idx_ptr = malloc(sizeof(int));
                HashMap_Put(history_indices, g_current_feature, idx_ptr);
            }
            *idx_ptr = idx;
            return;
        } 
    }
}

char* PromptUser(const char* window_list[], const char* window_title, int count, int height, int width, int item_height,
                 RefreshCallback refresh_callback, 
                 EnterCallback enter_callback,
                 CtrlEnterCallback ctrl_enter_callback,
                 TabCallback tab_callback,
                 RenderCallback render_callback,
                 EditCallback edit_callback,
                 FilterCallback filter_callback) {
    HINSTANCE hInstance = GetModuleHandle(NULL);
    selection_complete = false;
    output_buffer[0] = '\0';
    g_initial_items = window_list;
    g_initial_count = count;
    g_refresh_callback = refresh_callback;
    g_enter_callback = enter_callback;
    g_ctrl_enter_callback = ctrl_enter_callback;
    g_tab_callback = tab_callback;
    g_render_callback = render_callback;
    g_edit_callback = edit_callback;
    g_filter_callback = filter_callback;
    g_title = window_title;
    g_current_window_height = height;
    g_current_window_width = width;
    g_current_feature = (char*)window_title + 4;
    struct feature_data* data = GetFeatureData(g_current_feature);
    if (data) {
        data->name = _strdup(g_current_feature);
        data->items = window_list;
        data->item_count = count;
        data->refresh_callback = refresh_callback;
        data->enter_callback = enter_callback;
        data->ctrl_enter_callback = ctrl_enter_callback;
        data->tab_callback = tab_callback;
        data->render_callback = render_callback;
        data->filter_match = NULL;
        
        printf("[REGISTRY] Registered feature: %s (%d items)\n", data->name, data->item_count);
    }
    
    SetMode(MODE_INSERT);

    WindowLayout layout;
    CalculateWindowMetrics(count, &layout, height, width, item_height);
    g_current_window_height = layout.h;
    g_current_window_width = layout.w;
    
    HFONT hCustomFont = CreateCustomNerdFont(FontSize, UsedFont);

    hPopupWnd = CreateWindowExA(
        WS_EX_TOPMOST | WS_EX_TOOLWINDOW | WS_EX_LAYERED | WS_EX_COMPOSITED,
        "QuickSwitcherModalClass", NULL, WS_POPUP | WS_CLIPCHILDREN, 
        layout.x, layout.y, layout.w, layout.h, NULL, NULL, hInstance, NULL
    );
    
    SetTimer(hPopupWnd, 999, 50, NULL);

    if (hPopupWnd == NULL) {
        DWORD err = GetLastError();
        if (err == 1407) {
        }
        DeleteObject(hCustomFont);
        return NULL;
    }

    CreateChildControls(hPopupWnd, hCustomFont, window_list, count, &layout);
    
    SetLayeredWindowAttributes(hPopupWnd, 0, 245, LWA_ALPHA);

    ForceWindowFocus(hPopupWnd, hEditControl);


    printf("[DEBUG-UI] Entering local message modal loop...\n");
    MSG msg;
    int loop_counter = 0;
    while (!selection_complete) {
        if (PeekMessage(&msg, NULL, 0, 0, PM_REMOVE)) {
            loop_counter++;
            TranslateMessage(&msg);
            DispatchMessage(&msg);
        }
    }
    printf("[DEBUG-UI] Left local loop. Processed %d messages.\n", loop_counter);

    if (hPopupWnd != NULL && IsWindow(hPopupWnd)) {
        KillTimer(hPopupWnd, 999);
        DestroyWindow(hPopupWnd);
        hPopupWnd = NULL;
    }
    ResetNumberShortcutState();
    
    printf("[DEBUG-UI] Flushing trailing queue entries...\n");
    while (PeekMessage(&msg, NULL, 0, 0, PM_REMOVE)) {
        TranslateMessage(&msg);
        DispatchMessage(&msg);
    }

    DeleteObject(hCustomFont); 
    selection_complete = false;
    g_refresh_callback = NULL;
    g_enter_callback = NULL;
    g_ctrl_enter_callback = NULL;
    g_tab_callback = NULL;
    g_render_callback = NULL;
    g_edit_callback = NULL;
    g_filter_callback = NULL;
    
    printf("[DEBUG-UI] Returning from PromptUser. Buffer Length: %zu\n", strlen(output_buffer));
    return (strlen(output_buffer) > 0) ? output_buffer : NULL;
}
char* SimplePromptUser(const char* prompt, const char* window_title,int height, int width, int item_height) {
    HINSTANCE hInstance = GetModuleHandle(NULL);
    selection_complete = false;
    output_buffer[0] = '\0';
    g_title = window_title;
    g_current_window_height = height;
    g_current_window_width = width;
    g_current_feature = (char*)window_title + 4;
    
    SetMode(MODE_INSERT);

    WindowLayout layout;
    CalculateWindowMetrics(1, &layout, height, width, item_height);
    g_current_window_height = layout.h;
    g_current_window_width = layout.w;
    
    HFONT hCustomFont = CreateCustomNerdFont(FontSize, UsedFont);

    hPopupWnd = CreateWindowExA(
        WS_EX_TOPMOST | WS_EX_TOOLWINDOW | WS_EX_LAYERED, 
        "QuickSwitcherModalClass", NULL, WS_POPUP | WS_CLIPCHILDREN, 
        layout.x, layout.y, layout.w, layout.h, NULL, NULL, hInstance, NULL
    );
    
    
    SetTimer(hPopupWnd, 999, 50, NULL);

    if (hPopupWnd == NULL) {
        DWORD err = GetLastError();
        if (err == 1407) {
        }
        DeleteObject(hCustomFont);
        return NULL;
    }

    SimpleCreateChildControls(hPopupWnd, hCustomFont, prompt, 1, &layout);
    
    SetLayeredWindowAttributes(hPopupWnd, 0, 245, LWA_ALPHA);

    ForceWindowFocus(hPopupWnd, hEditControl);
    
    MSG msg;
    int loop_counter = 0;
    while (!selection_complete) {
        if (PeekMessage(&msg, NULL, 0, 0, PM_REMOVE)) {
            loop_counter++;
            TranslateMessage(&msg);
            DispatchMessage(&msg);
        }
    }

    if (hPopupWnd != NULL && IsWindow(hPopupWnd)) {
        KillTimer(hPopupWnd, 999);
        DestroyWindow(hPopupWnd);
        hPopupWnd = NULL;
    }
    
    while (PeekMessage(&msg, NULL, 0, 0, PM_REMOVE)) {
        TranslateMessage(&msg);
        DispatchMessage(&msg);
    }

    DeleteObject(hCustomFont); 
    selection_complete = false;
    printf("[DEBUG-UI] Returning from SimplePromptUser. Buffer %s\n", output_buffer);
    return (strlen(output_buffer) > 0) ? output_buffer : NULL;
}

char* DisplayMessage(const char* message, int centerX, int centerY, int maxWidth, int maxHeight, int timeout) {
    HINSTANCE hInstance = GetModuleHandle(NULL);
    
    if (!message) return NULL;
    DestroyWindow(hDisplayMessage);
    KillTimer(hDisplayMessage,999);
    
    HDC hdc = GetDC(NULL);
    HFONT hFont = CreateCustomNerdFont(FontSize, UsedFont);
    HFONT hOldFont = (HFONT)SelectObject(hdc, hFont);
    
    RECT textRect = {0, 0, maxWidth - 30, maxHeight - 30};
    DrawTextA(hdc, message, -1, &textRect, DT_CENTER | DT_WORDBREAK | DT_CALCRECT);
    
    SelectObject(hdc, hOldFont);
    DeleteObject(hFont);
    ReleaseDC(NULL, hdc);
    
    int padding = 30;
    int winWidth = textRect.right + padding;
    int winHeight = textRect.bottom + padding;
    
    if (winWidth > maxWidth) winWidth = maxWidth;
    if (winHeight > maxHeight) winHeight = maxHeight;
    
    FadeWindowData* pWndData = (FadeWindowData*)malloc(sizeof(FadeWindowData));
    if (!pWndData) return NULL;

    pWndData->message = _strdup(message);
    pWndData->hFont = CreateCustomNerdFont(FontSize, UsedFont);
    pWndData->alpha = 245;
    g_timeout = timeout * 20;

    int topLeftX = centerX - (winWidth / 2);
    int topLeftY = centerY - (winHeight / 2);

    HWND hPopupWnd = CreateWindowExA(
        WS_EX_TOPMOST | WS_EX_TOOLWINDOW | WS_EX_LAYERED | WS_EX_NOACTIVATE, 
        "DisplayMessageModalClass", NULL, WS_POPUP, 
        topLeftX, topLeftY, winWidth, winHeight, NULL, NULL, hInstance, pWndData
    );

    if (hPopupWnd == NULL) {
        printf("[DEBUG-UI] ERROR: CreateWindowExA failed. GetLastError: %lu\n", GetLastError());
        DeleteObject(pWndData->hFont);
        free(pWndData);
        return NULL;
    }

    SetLayeredWindowAttributes(hPopupWnd, 0, pWndData->alpha, LWA_ALPHA);
    
    ShowWindow(hPopupWnd, SW_SHOWNOACTIVATE);
    UpdateWindow(hPopupWnd);

    SetTimer(hPopupWnd, 999, 50, NULL);
    
    return NULL; 
}

char* EditTextPopup(const char* initial_text, int centerX, int centerY, int maxWidth, int maxHeight) {
    HINSTANCE hInstance = GetModuleHandle(NULL);
    int index = g_SelectedIndex;
    
    if (!initial_text) return NULL;
    
    if (g_hEditPopup && IsWindow(g_hEditPopup)) {
        DestroyWindow(g_hEditPopup);
        g_hEditPopup = NULL;
    }
    
    // === MEASURE TEXT ===
    HDC hdc = GetDC(NULL);
    HFONT hFont = CreateCustomNerdFont(FontSize, UsedFont);
    HFONT hOldFont = (HFONT)SelectObject(hdc, hFont);
    
    // Measure the text to size the window
    RECT textRect = {0, 0, maxWidth - 40, maxHeight - 60};
    DrawTextA(hdc, initial_text, -1, &textRect, DT_LEFT | DT_WORDBREAK | DT_CALCRECT);
    
    SelectObject(hdc, hOldFont);
    DeleteObject(hFont);
    ReleaseDC(NULL, hdc);
    
    int padding = 30;
    int editHeight = 40;
    int winWidth = textRect.right + padding + 20;
    int winHeight = textRect.bottom + padding + editHeight + 20;
    
    if (winWidth > maxWidth) winWidth = maxWidth;
    if (winHeight > maxHeight) winHeight = maxHeight;
    if (winWidth < 200) winWidth = 200;
    if (winHeight < 100) winHeight = 100;
    
    // === CREATE DATA ===
    EditPopupData* pData = (EditPopupData*)malloc(sizeof(EditPopupData));
    if (!pData) return NULL;
    
    pData->initial_text = _strdup(initial_text);
    pData->result = NULL;
    pData->hFont = CreateCustomNerdFont(FontSize, UsedFont);
    pData->alpha = 255;
    pData->hEdit = NULL;
    pData->consumed_trigger_e = false;
    
    int topLeftX = centerX - (winWidth / 2);
    int topLeftY = centerY - (winHeight / 2);
    
    HWND hPopupWnd = CreateWindowExA(
        WS_EX_TOPMOST | WS_EX_TOOLWINDOW | WS_EX_LAYERED | WS_EX_NOACTIVATE,
        "EditPopupClass", NULL, WS_POPUP,
        topLeftX, topLeftY, winWidth, winHeight,
        NULL, NULL, hInstance, pData
    );
    
    if (hPopupWnd == NULL) {
        printf("[ERROR] EditPopup creation failed\n");
        DeleteObject(pData->hFont);
        free(pData);
        return NULL;
    }
    
    g_hEditPopup = hPopupWnd;
    g_EditData = pData;
    
    SetLayeredWindowAttributes(hPopupWnd, 0, pData->alpha, LWA_ALPHA);
    
    ShowWindow(hPopupWnd, SW_SHOWNOACTIVATE);
    UpdateWindow(hPopupWnd);
    
    UpdateTextCaret(g_ActiveEditControl);
    
    SetTimer(hPopupWnd, 999, 50, NULL);
    
    MSG msg;
    while (IsWindow(hPopupWnd) && g_EditData && g_EditData->result == NULL) {
        if (PeekMessage(&msg, NULL, 0, 0, PM_REMOVE)) {
            TranslateMessage(&msg);
            DispatchMessage(&msg);
        } else {
            Sleep(10);
        }
    }
    
    char* result = NULL;
    if (g_EditData && g_EditData->result) {
        result = _strdup(g_EditData->result);
    }
    
    if (hPopupWnd && IsWindow(hPopupWnd)) {
        DestroyWindow(hPopupWnd);
    }
    g_hEditPopup = NULL;
    g_EditData = NULL;
    g_SelectedIndex = index;

    
    return result;
}

HHOOK hKeyboardHook = NULL;
#include <shlwapi.h>
#pragma comment(lib, "Shlwapi.lib")

int InitializeTrayIcon() {
    HINSTANCE hInstance = GetModuleHandle(NULL);
    if (TRAY) {
        WNDCLASS wc = {0};
        wc.lpfnWndProc = WndProc;
        wc.hInstance = hInstance;
        wc.lpszClassName = "TrayAppClass";

        if (!RegisterClass(&wc)) return -1;

        
        hGlobalTrayWnd = CreateWindow(
            wc.lpszClassName, "TrayApp", 
            WS_OVERLAPPEDWINDOW, 
            CW_USEDEFAULT, CW_USEDEFAULT, 0, 0, 
            NULL, NULL, hInstance, NULL
        );

        if (!hGlobalTrayWnd) return -1;
    }
    return 0;
}


LRESULT CALLBACK LowLevelKeyboardProc(int nCode, WPARAM wParam, LPARAM lParam) {
    if (nCode < 0) {
        return CallNextHookEx(NULL, nCode, wParam, lParam);
    }
    
    if (nCode == HC_ACTION && (wParam == WM_KEYDOWN || wParam == WM_SYSKEYDOWN)) {
        KBDLLHOOKSTRUCT* pKeyInfo = (KBDLLHOOKSTRUCT*)lParam;

        bool alt_down   = (pKeyInfo->flags & LLKHF_ALTDOWN) || (GetKeyState(VK_MENU) & 0x8000);
        bool ctrl_down  = (GetKeyState(VK_CONTROL) & 0x8000);
        bool shift_down = (GetKeyState(VK_SHIFT) & 0x8000) || 
                          (GetKeyState(VK_LSHIFT) & 0x8000) || 
                          (GetKeyState(VK_RSHIFT) & 0x8000);
        
        double seconds = GetUniversalTimeSeconds();
        if (seconds >= 300) {
            ResetUniversalTimer();
            printf("SUPPOSED TO CLEAR FILE");
        }

        HWND hForeground = GetForegroundWindow();
        char fgTitle[256] = {0};
        if (hForeground != NULL) {
            UI_GetText(hForeground, fgTitle, sizeof(fgTitle));
        }

        int pressed_vk = (int)pKeyInfo->vkCode;
        int modifiers = HOTKEY_MOD_NONE;
        if (alt_down) modifiers |= HOTKEY_MOD_ALT;
        if (shift_down) modifiers |= HOTKEY_MOD_SHIFT;
        if (ctrl_down) modifiers |= HOTKEY_MOD_CTRL;

        if (ProcessHotkey(G_HOTKEY_BINDINGS, HOTKEY_BINDING_COUNT, 
                          pressed_vk, modifiers, fgTitle)) {
            return 1;
        }

        MacroAction matchedAction = MACRO_NONE;
        int matchedDelay = 0;
        const char* snippet_text = NULL;
        
        MotionAction vimAction = MOTION_NONE;
        const char* matchedSequence = NULL;
        int typedCount = 0;
        if (CheckVimMotion(pressed_vk, modifiers, &vimAction, &matchedSequence, &typedCount)) {
            ExecuteMotion(vimAction, matchedSequence, typedCount);
            return 1;
        }
        if (CheckTextSnippetBindings(pressed_vk, modifiers, &snippet_text)) {
            if (snippet_text != NULL) {
                CopyToClipboard(snippet_text);
                return 1;
            }
        }
        if (CheckMacroBindings(pressed_vk, modifiers, &matchedAction, &matchedDelay)) {
            if (matchedAction != MACRO_NONE && hGlobalTrayWnd != NULL) {
                PostMessage(hGlobalTrayWnd, WM_TRIGGER_UNIVERSAL_MACRO, (WPARAM)matchedAction, (LPARAM)matchedDelay);
                return 1;
            }
        }
        if (CheckAppHotkeyBindings(pressed_vk, modifiers)) {
            return 1;
        }
        if (CheckMiscHotkeyBindings(pressed_vk, modifiers)) {
            return 1;
        }
        if (CheckQuickLinkHotkeyBindings(pressed_vk, modifiers)) {
            return 1;
        }
        if (HandleFocusSwitcher(pressed_vk, modifiers, fgTitle)) {
            return 1;
        }
        if (HandleMusicPlayer(pressed_vk, modifiers, fgTitle)) {
            return 1;
        }
        if (HandleVideoPlayer(pressed_vk, modifiers, fgTitle)) {
            return 1;
        }
        if (HandleBulkKillProcess(pressed_vk, modifiers, fgTitle)) {
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return CallNextHookEx(NULL, nCode, wParam, lParam);
}

void RunHotKeyMessageLoop() {
    HINSTANCE hInstance = GetModuleHandle(NULL);
    
    hKeyboardHook = SetWindowsHookEx(WH_KEYBOARD_LL, LowLevelKeyboardProc, hInstance, 0);
    if (!hKeyboardHook) {
        printf("[ERROR] Failed to install keyboard hook! Error: %lu\n", GetLastError());
        return;
    }

    MSG msg;
    while (GetMessage(&msg, NULL, 0, 0) > 0) {
        TranslateMessage(&msg);
        DispatchMessage(&msg);
    }

    if (hKeyboardHook) UnhookWindowsHookEx(hKeyboardHook);
}

int main() {
    StartUniversalTimer();
    InitializeTrayIcon();
    initialize_app_cache();
    feature_history = HashMap_Create(32);
    feature_data = HashMap_Create(32);
    
    struct GdiplusStartupInput gdiplusStartupInput;
    gdiplusStartupInput.GdiplusVersion = 1;
    gdiplusStartupInput.DebugEventCallback = NULL;
    gdiplusStartupInput.SuppressBackgroundThread = FALSE;
    gdiplusStartupInput.SuppressExternalCodecs = FALSE;
    GdiplusStartup(&g_gdiplusToken, &gdiplusStartupInput, NULL);

    
    WNDCLASSA wc = {0};
    wc.lpfnWndProc = PopupWndProc;
    wc.hInstance = GetModuleHandle(NULL);
    wc.hCursor = LoadCursor(NULL, IDC_ARROW);
    wc.hbrBackground = GetStockObject(NULL_BRUSH);
    wc.lpszClassName = "QuickSwitcherModalClass";
    
    if (RegisterClassA(&wc)) {
        printf("[SYSTEM-BOOT] QuickSwitcherModalClass registered successfully with OS.\n");
    } else {
        printf("[SYSTEM-BOOT] CRITICAL WARNING: Failed to register window class! Error: %lu\n", GetLastError());
    }
    // NEW: Register your dedicated standalone notification class
    WNDCLASSA wcFade = {0};
    wcFade.lpfnWndProc = DisplayMessageProc;
    wcFade.hInstance = GetModuleHandle(NULL);
    wcFade.hCursor = LoadCursor(NULL, IDC_ARROW);
    wcFade.hbrBackground = (HBRUSH)(COLOR_BACKGROUND + 1); 
    wcFade.lpszClassName = "DisplayMessageModalClass";

    if (RegisterClassA(&wcFade)) {
        printf("[SYSTEM-BOOT] DisplayMessageClass registered successfully with OS.\n");
    } else {
        printf("[SYSTEM-BOOT] WARNING: Failed to register FadeNotificationClass! Error: %lu\n", GetLastError());
    }
    WNDCLASSA editPopupWc = {0};
    editPopupWc.lpfnWndProc = EditPopupProc;
    editPopupWc.hInstance = GetModuleHandle(NULL);
    editPopupWc.hCursor = LoadCursor(NULL, IDC_ARROW);
    editPopupWc.hbrBackground = CreateSolidBrush(COLOR_BG);
    editPopupWc.lpszClassName = "EditPopupClass";
    RegisterClassA(&editPopupWc);
    
    if (RegisterClassA(&editPopupWc)) {
        printf("[SYSTEM-BOOT] DisplayMessageClass registered successfully with OS.\n");
    } else {
        printf("[SYSTEM-BOOT] WARNING: Failed to register FadeNotificationClass! Error: %lu\n", GetLastError());
    }
    
    for (int i = 0; i < FILE_ENTRY_COUNT; i++) {
        SanitizeFile(G_FILE_ENTRIES[i].path);
    }

    for (int i = 0; i < FILE_ENTRY_COUNT; i++) {
        G_FILE_ENTRIES[i].mf = (MappedFile*)malloc(sizeof(MappedFile));
        const char* path = G_FILE_ENTRIES[i].path;
        
        
        if (!FileInit(path, G_FILE_ENTRIES[i].mf, 0)) {
            printf("[SYSTEM] Failed to open %s. Creating new.\n", G_FILE_ENTRIES[i].name);
            HANDLE hFile = CreateFileA(path, GENERIC_WRITE, 0, NULL, CREATE_NEW, FILE_ATTRIBUTE_NORMAL, NULL);
            if (hFile != INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) {
                CloseHandle(hFile);
                FileInit(path, G_FILE_ENTRIES[i].mf, 0); 
            }
        }
    }

    printf("[SYSTEM] Low-Level Hotkey Services Running. GDI+ Online.\n");
    RunHotKeyMessageLoop();

    if (g_gdiplusToken != 0) {
        GdiplusShutdown(g_gdiplusToken);
    }
    
    for (int i = 0; i < FILE_ENTRY_COUNT; i++) {
        if (G_FILE_ENTRIES[i].mf != NULL) {
            FileClose(G_FILE_ENTRIES[i].mf);
            free(G_FILE_ENTRIES[i].mf);
            G_FILE_ENTRIES[i].mf = NULL;
        }
    }
    if (g_hNumFont) {
        DeleteObject(g_hNumFont);
        g_hNumFont = NULL;
    }
    if (g_hMainFont) {
        DeleteObject(g_hMainFont);
        g_hMainFont = NULL;
    }
    
    return 0;
}



/* === FILE: minecraft.h === */
// minecraft.h
#ifndef MINECRAFT_H
#define MINECRAFT_H

#include <windows.h>
#include <stdio.h>

static void minecraft_type_command(const char* command) {
    // Open chat with T
    INPUT t[2] = {0};
    t[0].type = INPUT_KEYBOARD; 
    t[0].ki.wVk = 'T';
    t[1].type = INPUT_KEYBOARD; 
    t[1].ki.wVk = 'T'; 
    t[1].ki.dwFlags = KEYEVENTF_KEYUP;
    SendInput(2, t, sizeof(INPUT));
    Sleep(60);

    // Type the command
    for (int i = 0; command[i]; i++) {
        INPUT ch[2] = {0};
        ch[0].type = INPUT_KEYBOARD; 
        ch[0].ki.wVk = command[i];
        ch[1].type = INPUT_KEYBOARD; 
        ch[1].ki.wVk = command[i]; 
        ch[1].ki.dwFlags = KEYEVENTF_KEYUP;
        SendInput(2, ch, sizeof(INPUT));
    }

    // Press Enter
    INPUT enter[2] = {0};
    enter[0].type = INPUT_KEYBOARD; 
    enter[0].ki.wVk = VK_RETURN;
    enter[1].type = INPUT_KEYBOARD; 
    enter[1].ki.wVk = VK_RETURN; 
    enter[1].ki.dwFlags = KEYEVENTF_KEYUP;
    SendInput(2, enter, sizeof(INPUT));
}

#endif // MINECRAFT_H


/* === FILE: modal.h === */
// modal.h
#ifndef MODAL_H
#define MODAL_H

#include <windows.h>
#include <stdbool.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include "common_ui.h"
#include "types.h"

typedef enum {
    MODE_NORMAL,
    MODE_INSERT,
    MODE_VISUAL,
} EditorMode;

static EditorMode g_current_mode = MODE_INSERT;
static bool g_vim_mode_enabled = true;
static const char* g_mode_names[] = { "NORMAL", "INSERT", "VISUAL" };
const char* g_vim_mode = "INSERT";
int last_selection = 0;

static inline void SetMode(EditorMode mode) {
    g_current_mode = mode;
    g_vim_mode = g_mode_names[mode];
    
    extern HWND g_hEditPopup;
    bool in_popup = (g_hEditPopup != NULL && IsWindow(g_hEditPopup));
    
    if (!in_popup) {
        if (strcmp(g_vim_mode,"INSERT") == 0 ) {
          last_selection = UI_GetSelection(hListBox);
          UI_SetSelection(hListBox,-1, g_SelectedIndex);
        } else if (strcmp(g_vim_mode,"NORMAL") == 0) {
          if (g_TotalMatches != 0) {
              if (last_selection <= 0) {
                  UI_SetSelection(hListBox,0, g_SelectedIndex);
              } else {
                  UI_SetSelection(hListBox,last_selection, g_SelectedIndex);
              }
          }
        }
    }
    
    if (g_ActiveEditControl) {
        UpdateTextCaret(g_ActiveEditControl);
    }
    
    extern HWND hPopupWnd;
    if (hPopupWnd != NULL && IsWindow(hPopupWnd)) {
        InvalidateRect(hPopupWnd, NULL, FALSE);
        UpdateWindow(hPopupWnd);
    }
    if (g_hEditPopup != NULL && IsWindow(g_hEditPopup)) {
        InvalidateRect(g_hEditPopup, NULL, FALSE);
        UpdateWindow(g_hEditPopup);
    }
}


static inline const char* GetModeName(void) {
    return g_mode_names[g_current_mode];
}

static inline EditorMode GetMode(void) { return g_current_mode; }
static inline bool IsNormalMode(void) { return g_current_mode == MODE_NORMAL; }
static inline bool IsInsertMode(void) { return g_current_mode == MODE_INSERT; }
static inline bool IsVisualMode(void) { return g_current_mode == MODE_VISUAL; }

#endif


/* === FILE: monitor_focus.h === */
#ifndef MONITOR_FOCUS_H
#define MONITOR_FOCUS_H

#include <windows.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <string.h>
#include "focus_switcher.h"
#include "types.h"


static inline BOOL CALLBACK EnumMonitorWindowsProc(HWND hwnd, LPARAM lParam) {
    MonitorWindowList* myList = (MonitorWindowList*)lParam;

    if (!IsWindowVisible(hwnd) || GetWindowTextLengthA(hwnd) == 0) {
        return TRUE;
    }

    char className[256];
    GetClassNameA(hwnd, className, sizeof(className));
    if (strcmp(className, "Progman") == 0 || strcmp(className, "WorkerW") == 0 || strcmp(className, "Shell_TrayWnd") == 0) {
        return TRUE; 
    }

    HMONITOR hMonitor = MonitorFromWindow(hwnd, MONITOR_DEFAULTTONULL);
    if (hMonitor != myList->targetMonitor) {
        return TRUE; 
    }

    if (myList->count >= myList->capacity) {
        return FALSE;
    }

    MonitorWindowData* currentItem = &myList->items[myList->count];
    currentItem->hwnd = hwnd;
    GetWindowTextA(hwnd, currentItem->title, sizeof(currentItem->title));
    

    myList->count++;
    return TRUE;
}

static BOOL CALLBACK EnumMonitorsProc(HMONITOR hMonitor, HDC hdcMonitor, LPRECT lprcMonitor, LPARAM lParam) {
    MonitorSearch *search = (MonitorSearch *)lParam;
    search->currentIndex++;
    
    if (search->currentIndex == search->targetIndex) {
        search->foundMonitor = hMonitor;
        return FALSE;
    }
    return TRUE;
}

static void ScanWindowsOnMonitor(HMONITOR targetMonitor) {
    if (!targetMonitor) {
        return;
    }

    MonitorWindowList myList;
    myList.targetMonitor = targetMonitor;
    myList.count = 0;
    myList.capacity = 64;

    EnumWindows(EnumMonitorWindowsProc, (LPARAM)&myList);

    if (myList.count == 0) {
    } else {
        FocusWindow(myList.items[0].hwnd);
    }
}

static inline void TriggerMonitorFocusMain() {
    MonitorSearch search = {0};
    search.targetIndex = 1; 
    search.currentIndex = 0;
    search.foundMonitor = NULL;

    EnumDisplayMonitors(NULL, NULL, EnumMonitorsProc, (LPARAM)&search);

    if (search.foundMonitor != NULL) {
        ScanWindowsOnMonitor(search.foundMonitor);
    } else {
    }
}

static inline void TriggerMonitorFocusSide() {
    MonitorSearch search = {0};
    search.targetIndex = 2; 
    search.currentIndex = 0;
    search.foundMonitor = NULL;

    EnumDisplayMonitors(NULL, NULL, EnumMonitorsProc, (LPARAM)&search);

    if (search.foundMonitor != NULL) {
        ScanWindowsOnMonitor(search.foundMonitor);
    } else {
    }
}
