/* === FILE: todo.h === */
#ifndef TODO_H
#define TODO_H

#include <ctype.h>
#include <windows.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <stdbool.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <winsock.h>
#include "helpers.h"
#include "modal.h"
#include "types.h"
#include "config.h"
#include "common_ui.h"

static inline COLORREF GetTagColor(const char* tag_text) {
    if (!tag_text || strlen(tag_text) == 0) return COLOR_TEXT;
    
    for (int i = 0; i < TODO_CONFIG_FLAGS_COUNT; i++) {
        if (TODO_CONFIG_FLAGS_ENTRIES[i].tag_label) {
            if (strstr(tag_text, TODO_CONFIG_FLAGS_ENTRIES[i].tag_label) != NULL) {
                return TODO_CONFIG_FLAGS_ENTRIES[i].color;
            }
        }
    }
    for (int i = 0; i < TODO_ALIAS_DICT_COUNT; i++) {
        if (strstr(tag_text, TODO_ALIAS_DICT[i].alias) != NULL) {
            return COLOR_TEXT;
        }
    }
    return COLOR_TEXT;
}

static void ParseTodoInput(const char* input, TodoAliasEntry* alias_out, TodoConfigEntry* config_out, char* clean_text, char tags_out[][64], int* tag_count) {
    if (!input) return;
    
    memset(alias_out, 0, sizeof(TodoAliasEntry));
    if (config_out) memset(config_out, 0, sizeof(TodoConfigEntry));
    clean_text[0] = '\0';
    *tag_count = 0;
    
    char input_copy[512];
    strncpy(input_copy, input, sizeof(input_copy) - 1);
    input_copy[sizeof(input_copy) - 1] = '\0';
    
    FoundFlag alias_matches[10];
    int alias_match_count = SearchFlagsInText(input_copy, TODO_ALIAS_FLAGS, TODO_ALIAS_FLAGS_COUNT, sizeof(GenericFlagEntry), alias_matches, 10);
    
    for (int i = 0; i < alias_match_count && *tag_count < 10; i++) {
        char* param = GetFlagParameter(input_copy, alias_matches[i].index);
        if (param && strlen(param) > 0) {
            int alias_idx = -1;
            for (int j = 0; j < TODO_ALIAS_DICT_COUNT; j++) {
                if (strcmp(alias_matches[i].flag, TODO_ALIAS_DICT[j].alias) == 0) {
                    alias_idx = j;
                    break;
                }
            }
            if (alias_idx >= 0) {
                snprintf(tags_out[*tag_count], 64, "%s%s %s%s", 
                    TODO_TAG_OPEN, TODO_ALIAS_DICT[alias_idx].alias_label, param, TODO_TAG_CLOSE);
                (*tag_count)++;
            }
        }
    }
    
    FoundFlag config_matches[10];
    int config_match_count = SearchFlagsInText(input_copy, TODO_CONFIG_FLAGS, TODO_CONFIG_FLAGS_COUNT, sizeof(GenericFlagEntry), config_matches, 10);
    
    for (int i = 0; i < config_match_count && *tag_count < 10; i++) {
        for (int j = 0; j < TODO_CONFIG_FLAGS_COUNT; j++) {
            if (strcmp(config_matches[i].flag, TODO_CONFIG_FLAGS[j].flag) == 0) {
                if (TODO_CONFIG_FLAGS_ENTRIES[j].tag_label) {
                    snprintf(tags_out[*tag_count], 64, "%s%s%s", 
                        TODO_TAG_OPEN, TODO_CONFIG_FLAGS_ENTRIES[j].tag_label, TODO_TAG_CLOSE);
                    (*tag_count)++;
                }
                if (config_out && TODO_CONFIG_FLAGS_ENTRIES[j].is_alias) {
                    char* param = GetFlagParameter(input_copy, config_matches[i].index);
                    if (param && strlen(param) > 0) {
                        memcpy((void*)config_out->internal_name, TODO_CONFIG_FLAGS_ENTRIES[j].internal_name, sizeof(config_out->internal_name) - 1);
                        snprintf(tags_out[*tag_count], 64, "%s%s %s%s", 
                            TODO_TAG_OPEN, TODO_CONFIG_FLAGS_ENTRIES[j].internal_name, param, TODO_TAG_CLOSE);
                        (*tag_count)++;
                    }
                }
                break;
            }
        }
    }
    
    char clean[512] = {0};
    char* token = strtok(input_copy, " ");
    bool first = true;
    
    while (token != NULL) {
        bool is_flag = false;
        
        for (int i = 0; i < TODO_ALIAS_FLAGS_COUNT; i++) {
            if (strcmp(token, TODO_ALIAS_FLAGS[i].flag) == 0) {
                is_flag = true;
                token = strtok(NULL, " ");
                break;
            }
        }
        
        if (!is_flag) {
            for (int i = 0; i < TODO_CONFIG_FLAGS_COUNT; i++) {
                if (strcmp(token, TODO_CONFIG_FLAGS[i].flag) == 0) {
                    is_flag = true;
                    if (TODO_CONFIG_FLAGS_ENTRIES[i].is_alias) {
                        token = strtok(NULL, " ");
                    }
                    break;
                }
            }
        }
        
        if (!is_flag && token[0] != '-') {
            if (first) {
                strcpy(clean, token);
                first = false;
            } else {
                strcat(clean, " ");
                strcat(clean, token);
            }
        }
        
        token = strtok(NULL, " ");
    }
    
    strncpy(clean_text, clean, 511);
    clean_text[511] = '\0';
}

static void ParseTodoLine(const char* line, char* clean_text, char tags_out[][64], int* tag_count) {
    clean_text[0] = '\0';
    *tag_count = 0;
    
    char line_copy[512];
    strncpy(line_copy, line, sizeof(line_copy) - 1);
    line_copy[sizeof(line_copy) - 1] = '\0';
    
    char* delimiter = strstr(line_copy, TODO_ENTRY_DELIMITER);
    if (delimiter) {
        *delimiter = '\0';
        strncpy(clean_text, line_copy, 511);
        clean_text[511] = '\0';
        
        char* tag_part = delimiter + strlen(TODO_ENTRY_DELIMITER);
        char* tag_token = strtok(tag_part, TODO_TAG_SEPARATOR);
        while (tag_token && *tag_count < 10) {
            strncpy(tags_out[*tag_count], tag_token, 63);
            tags_out[*tag_count][63] = '\0';
            (*tag_count)++;
            tag_token = strtok(NULL, TODO_TAG_SEPARATOR);
        }
    } else {
        strncpy(clean_text, line_copy, 511);
        clean_text[511] = '\0';
    }
}

static void AddTodoToFile(const char* text) {
    if (!text || strlen(text) == 0) return;
    
    char lines[100][512];
    int count = ReadMdFile(lines, TODO_FILE_PATH);
    
    char input_copy[512];
    strncpy(input_copy, text, sizeof(input_copy) - 1);
    input_copy[sizeof(input_copy) - 1] = '\0';
    
    TodoAliasEntry alias;
    TodoConfigEntry config;
    char clean_text[512];
    char tags[10][64];
    int tag_count = 0;
    
    ParseTodoInput(input_copy, &alias, &config, clean_text, tags, &tag_count);
    
    for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        char existing_clean[512];
        char existing_tags[10][64];
        int existing_tag_count = 0;
        ParseTodoLine(lines[i], existing_clean, existing_tags, &existing_tag_count);
        if (strcmp(existing_clean, clean_text) == 0) return;
    }
    
    char new_entry[512];
    new_entry[0] = '\0';
    strncpy(new_entry, clean_text, sizeof(new_entry) - 1);
    
    if (tag_count > 0) {
        strcat(new_entry, TODO_ENTRY_DELIMITER);
        for (int i = 0; i < tag_count; i++) {
            strcat(new_entry, tags[i]);
            if (i < tag_count - 1) {
                strcat(new_entry, TODO_TAG_SEPARATOR);
            }
        }
    }
    
    strncpy(lines[count], new_entry, sizeof(lines[count]) - 1);
    lines[count][sizeof(lines[count]) - 1] = '\0';
    count++;
    WriteMdFile(lines, count, TODO_FILE_PATH);
}

static void RemoveTodoItemFromFile(const char* item_to_remove) {
    FILE* infile = fopen(TODO_FILE_PATH, "r");
    if (!infile) return;

    FILE* outfile = fopen("todo.tmp", "w");
    if (!outfile) {
        fclose(infile);
        return;
    }

    char line[512];
    while (fgets(line, sizeof(line), infile)) {
        char clean_line[512];
        strncpy(clean_line, line, sizeof(clean_line) - 1);
        clean_line[sizeof(clean_line) - 1] = '\0';
        clean_line[strcspn(clean_line, "\r\n")] = '\0';

        char compare_text[512];
        strncpy(compare_text, clean_line, sizeof(compare_text) - 1);
        char* delimiter = strstr(compare_text, TODO_ENTRY_DELIMITER);
        if (delimiter) {
            *delimiter = '\0';
        }
        
        char clean_item[512];
        strncpy(clean_item, item_to_remove, sizeof(clean_item) - 1);
        char* item_delimiter = strstr(clean_item, TODO_ENTRY_DELIMITER);
        if (item_delimiter) {
            *item_delimiter = '\0';
        }
        
        if (strcmp(compare_text, clean_item) == 0) {
            printf("[TODO-MGR] Dropped file line item: \"%s\"\n", item_to_remove);
            continue; 
        }
        fprintf(outfile, "%s\n", clean_line);
    }

    fclose(infile);
    fclose(outfile);

    DeleteFileA(TODO_FILE_PATH);
    MoveFileA("todo.tmp", TODO_FILE_PATH);
}

static void todo_refresh_callback(const char* filter, HWND hListBox) {
    char todo_lines[100][512];
    int todo_count = ReadMdFile(todo_lines, TODO_FILE_PATH);
    
    const char* items[100];
    for (int i = 0; i < todo_count; i++) {
        items[i] = todo_lines[i];
    }
    PopulateListBox(hListBox, filter, items, todo_count, NULL);
}

static void todo_enter_callback(const char* selection) {
    char input[256] = {0};
    if (hEditControl != NULL) {
        UI_GetText(hEditControl, input, sizeof(input));
    }
    
    input[strcspn(input, "\r\n")] = '\0';
    size_t input_len = strlen(input);

    int selected_index = UI_GetSelection(hListBox);
    
    if (selected_index != LB_ERR && (input_len == 0 || strcmp(g_populated_cache[selected_index], input) == 0)) {
        const char* item_to_remove = g_populated_cache[selected_index];
        RemoveTodoItemFromFile(item_to_remove);
        todo_refresh_callback(input, hListBox);
    } 
    else if (input_len > 0) {
        AddTodoToFile(input);
        UI_SetText(hEditControl, "");
        todo_refresh_callback("", hListBox);
    }
}

static void todo_ctrlenter_callback(const char* selection) {
    int selected_index = UI_GetSelection(hListBox);
    
    if (selected_index != -1) {
        const char* item_to_remove = g_populated_cache[selected_index];
        RemoveTodoItemFromFile(item_to_remove);
    } else {
        char input[256] = {0};
        UI_GetText(hEditControl, input, sizeof(input));
        AddTodoToFile(input);
        UI_SetText(hEditControl, "");
    }
    todo_refresh_callback(NULL, hListBox);
    selection_complete = false;
}

static void todo_render_callback(HDC hdc, const char* text, RECT* rect, BOOL is_selected, int itemIndex) {
    if (!text || !rect) return;
    
    char main_text[512] = {0};
    char tags[10][64];
    int tag_count = 0;
    
    ParseTodoLine(text, main_text, tags, &tag_count);
    
    RECT textRect = *rect;
    textRect.left += 10;
    textRect.top += 4;
    textRect.bottom -= 30;
    
    if (!is_selected) {
        SetTextColor(hdc, GetRainbowColor((float)itemIndex / 20.0f, 0.85f, 0.85f));
    }
    
    HFONT hOldFont = (HFONT)SelectObject(hdc, g_hMainFont);
    DrawTextA(hdc, main_text, -1, &textRect, DT_SINGLELINE | DT_VCENTER);
    
    if (tag_count > 0) {
        RECT tagRect = *rect;
        tagRect.left += 14;
        tagRect.top += 28;
        tagRect.bottom -= 4;
        
        HFONT hOldTagFont = (HFONT)SelectObject(hdc, g_hMainFont);
        
        int y_offset = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < tag_count; i++) {
            SetTextColor(hdc, GetTagColor(tags[i]));
            RECT singleTagRect = tagRect;
            singleTagRect.top += y_offset;
            DrawTextA(hdc, tags[i], -1, &singleTagRect, DT_LEFT | DT_TOP);
            y_offset += 18;
        }
        
        SelectObject(hdc, hOldTagFont);
    }
    
    SelectObject(hdc, hOldFont);
}

static void todo_edit_callback(const char* selection) {
    char* NewTodoName = EditTextPopup(selection, 960, 540, 1000, 300);
    
    if (NewTodoName != NULL) {
        WriteToCacheIndex(g_SelectedIndex, NewTodoName);
    }
    ReplaceLineInFile(TODO_FILE_PATH, g_SelectedIndex, NewTodoName);
}

void TriggerTodo(void) {
    char todo_lines[100][512];
    int count = ReadMdFile(todo_lines, TODO_FILE_PATH);

    const char* ui_list[100];
    for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        ui_list[i] = todo_lines[i];
    }

    char* selected_item = PromptUser(ui_list, "📝 Todo", count, 920, 650, 56,
                                     todo_refresh_callback,  
                                     todo_enter_callback,    
                                     todo_ctrlenter_callback, 
                                     NULL,                    
                                     todo_render_callback,
                                     todo_edit_callback,
                                     NULL);
}

#endif


/* === FILE: types.h === */
#ifndef TYPES_H
#define TYPES_H

#include <windows.h>
#include <stdbool.h>
#include <string.h>


typedef void (*RefreshCallback)(const char* filter, HWND hListBox);
typedef void (*EnterCallback)(const char* selection);
typedef void (*CtrlEnterCallback)(const char* selection);
typedef void (*TabCallback)(void);
typedef void (*RenderCallback)(HDC hdc, const char* text, RECT* rect, BOOL is_selected, int itemIndex);
typedef void (*EditCallback)(const char*);
typedef void (*FilterCallback)(const char* item,const char* filter);

char* PromptUser(const char* window_list[], const char* window_title, int count, 
                 int height, int width, int item_height, 
                 RefreshCallback refresh_callback, 
                 EnterCallback enter_callback,
                 CtrlEnterCallback ctrl_enter_callback,
                 TabCallback tab_callback,
                 RenderCallback render_callback,
                 EditCallback edit_callback,
                 FilterCallback filter_callback);

#define ImportantPromptUser(window_list,window_title,count,height,width,item_height,refresh_callback,enter_callback,edit_callback) PromptUser(window_list,window_title,count,height,width,item_height,refresh_callback,enter_callback,NULL,NULL,NULL,edit_callback,NULL)


char* SimplePromptUser(const char* prompt, const char* window_title,int height, int width, int item_height);

char* DisplayMessage(const char* message, int centerX, int centerY, int width, int height,int timeout);

char* EditTextPopup(const char* initial_text, int centerX, int centerY, int maxWidth, int maxHeight);

void SaveRegionToPNG(int x1, int y1, int x2, int y2, const char* fullPath);

void UpdateTextCaret(HWND hwndEditControl);

LRESULT CALLBACK EditSubclassProc(HWND hwnd, UINT msg, WPARAM wParam, LPARAM lParam, UINT_PTR uIdSubclass, DWORD_PTR dwRefData);

static inline const char* GetFilePath(const char* name) {
    for (int i = 0; i < FILE_ENTRY_COUNT; i++) {
        if (strcmp(G_FILE_ENTRIES[i].name, name) == 0) {
            return G_FILE_ENTRIES[i].path;
        }
    }
    return NULL;
}

/* === FILE: video_player.h === */
#ifndef VIDEO_PLAYER_H
#define VIDEO_PLAYER_H

#include <windows.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <shlwapi.h>
#include "config.h"
#include "file_search.h"
#include "app_launcher.h"
#include "types.h"

static void video_refresh_callback(const char* filter, HWND hListBox) {
    ClearFileList();
    GetFilesRecursive(VideosPath, false);
    
    const char** items = (const char**)malloc(GetFileCount() * sizeof(char*));
    if (!items) return;
    for (int i = 0; i < GetFileCount(); i++) {
        items[i] = G_FILES_WITH_TIME[i].name;
    }
    PopulateListBox(hListBox, filter, items, GetFileCount(), NULL);
    free(items);
}

static void video_enter_callback(const char* selection) {
    if (selection == NULL || strlen(selection) == 0) return;
    
    HWND playerHwnd = FindWindow(NULL, "Video Viewer");
    char* result = SearchFileRecursive(VideosPath, selection);
    
    if (!result) return;
    
    if (playerHwnd) {
        COPYDATASTRUCT cds;
        cds.dwData = 0;
        cds.cbData = (DWORD)(strlen(result) + 1);
        cds.lpData = (PVOID)result;
        SendMessage(playerHwnd, WM_COPYDATA, (WPARAM)NULL, (LPARAM)&cds);
        printf("[VIDEO] Sent to existing player: %s\n", result);
    } else {
        char video_player_path[MAX_PATH] = {0};
        for (int i = 0; i < G_APP_COUNT; i++) {
            if (StrStrIA(G_APPS[i].name, "video_viewer") != NULL) {
                strncpy(video_player_path, G_APPS[i].path, sizeof(video_player_path) - 1);
                break;
            }
        }
        if (strlen(video_player_path) > 0) {
            char quoted_result[MAX_PATH + 3] = {0};
            snprintf(quoted_result, sizeof(quoted_result), "\"%s\"", result);
            launch_exe_with_args(video_player_path, quoted_result);
            printf("[VIDEO] Launched new player with: %s\n", result);
        }
    }
}
static bool HandleVideoPlayer(int vk_code, int modifiers, const char* current_title) {
    if (CheckAndBlockIfPopupActive(hPopupWnd)) return false;
    if (!VideoViewerAvailable) return false;
    
    for (int i = 0; i < VIDEO_PLAYER_COUNT; i++) {
        const VideoPlayerBinding* binding = &G_VIDEO_PLAYERS[i];
        if (binding->vk_code == vk_code && binding->modifiers == modifiers) {
            HWND playerHwnd = FindWindow(NULL, "Video Viewer");
            
            if (binding->code != 69) {
                if (playerHwnd) {
                    PostMessage(playerHwnd, WM_USER + 1, binding->code, 0);
                    return true;
                } else {
                    return false;
                }
            } else {
                const char* prompt[1] = { "Search for video..." };
                
                ClearFileList();
                GetFilesRecursive(VideosPath,false);
                
                const char** initial_items = (const char**)malloc(GetFileCount() * sizeof(char*));
                if (!initial_items) return false;
                
                for (int i = 0; i < GetFileCount(); i++) {
                    initial_items[i] = G_FILES_WITH_TIME[i].name;
                }
                
                ImportantPromptUser(initial_items,"🎥 Viewer",GetFileCount(), 920, 650,32,
                                    video_refresh_callback,  
                                    video_enter_callback,    
                                    NULL);
                
                free((void*)initial_items);
                return true;
            }
        }
    }
    return false;
}

#endif

/* === FILE: vim_helpers.h === */
#include <windows.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include "common_ui.h"
#include "types.h"

static inline void DeleteCharUnderCursor(void) {
    if (!g_ActiveEditControl || !IsWindow(g_ActiveEditControl)) return;
    
    char text[512] = {0};
    UI_GetText(g_ActiveEditControl, text, sizeof(text));
    DWORD start, end;
    UI_Edit_GetSel(g_ActiveEditControl, &start, &end);
    
    int pos = (int)start;
    int len = (int)strlen(text);
    
    if (pos < len) {
        memmove(&text[pos], &text[pos + 1], len - pos);
        
        UI_SetText(g_ActiveEditControl,text);
        
        UI_SetCursorPosition(g_ActiveEditControl,pos);
    }
}

static inline void ExecuteEnter(void) {
    char text[512] = {0};
    
    char* selected = g_populated_cache[g_SelectedIndex];
    if (selected != NULL) {
        strncpy(text, selected, sizeof(text) - 1);
    } else if (g_ActiveEditControl && IsWindow(g_ActiveEditControl)) {
        UI_GetCurrentText(g_ActiveEditControl,text);
    }
    
    if (g_enter_callback != NULL && strlen(text) > 0) {
        g_enter_callback(text);
    }
    selection_complete = true;
}

static inline const char* GoToFirstListBoxItem(void) {
    if (!hListBox || !IsWindow(hListBox) || g_populated_count <= 0) return NULL;
    
    g_view_offset = 0;

    UI_SetSelection(hListBox,0,g_SelectedIndex);
    UI_LB_SetTopIndex(hListBox,0);
    return g_populated_cache[0];
}

static inline const char* GoToLastListBoxItem(void) {
    if (!hListBox || !IsWindow(hListBox) || g_populated_count <= 0) return NULL;
    
    int lastIndex = g_populated_count - 1;
    UI_SetSelection(hListBox,lastIndex,g_SelectedIndex);

    UI_LB_SetCaretIndex(hListBox, lastIndex, FALSE);

    return g_populated_cache[lastIndex];
}

static inline void SetCursorToStartOfText(void) {
    if (g_ActiveEditControl && IsWindow(g_ActiveEditControl)) {
        SetFocus(g_ActiveEditControl);
        UI_SetCursorPosition(g_ActiveEditControl,0);
    }
}

static inline void SetCursorToEndOfText(void) {
    if (g_ActiveEditControl && IsWindow(g_ActiveEditControl)) {
        SetFocus(g_ActiveEditControl);
        int len = GetWindowTextLengthA(g_ActiveEditControl);
        UI_SetCursorPosition(g_ActiveEditControl,len);
    }
}


static inline void DeleteInsideDelimiter(char open_char, char close_char, bool keep_mode) {
    if (!g_ActiveEditControl || !IsWindow(g_ActiveEditControl)) {
        return;
    }
    
    char text[512] = {0};
    UI_GetText(g_ActiveEditControl, text, sizeof(text));
    int len = (int)strlen(text);
    
    DWORD start_pos, end_pos;
    UI_Edit_GetSel(g_ActiveEditControl, &start_pos, &end_pos);
    int cursor = (int)start_pos;
    
    int start = -1, end = -1;
    
    // === FORWARDS: Cursor is inside or before the brackets ===
    int open_pos = -1;
    for (int i = cursor - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
        if (text[i] == open_char) {
            open_pos = i;
            break;
        }
    }
    
    int close_pos = -1;
    for (int i = cursor; i < len; i++) {
        if (text[i] == close_char) {
            close_pos = i;
            break;
        }
    }
    
    if (open_pos != -1 && close_pos != -1 && open_pos < close_pos) {
        start = open_pos;
        end = close_pos;
    } else {
        // === BACKWARDS: Cursor is after the closing bracket ===
        // Search left for close_char
        int close_left = -1;
        for (int i = cursor - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
            if (text[i] == close_char) {
                close_left = i;
                break;
            }
        }
        
        // Search left from close_char for matching open_char
        int open_left = -1;
        if (close_left != -1) {
            int depth = 0;
            for (int i = close_left - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
                if (text[i] == close_char) depth++;
                if (text[i] == open_char) {
                    if (depth == 0) {
                        open_left = i;
                        break;
                    } else {
                        depth--;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        
        if (open_left != -1 && close_left != -1 && open_left < close_left) {
            start = open_left;
            end = close_left;
        } else if (open_char == close_char) {
            // For quotes (same char), find the pair
            int found = 0;
            for (int i = 0; i < len; i++) {
                if (text[i] == open_char) {
                    if (found == 0) { start = i; found = 1; }
                    else { end = i; break; }
                }
            }
        } else {
            // Fallback: scan entire text
            int depth = 0;
            int first_open = -1;
            for (int i = 0; i < len; i++) {
                if (text[i] == open_char) {
                    if (first_open == -1) first_open = i;
                    depth++;
                }
                if (text[i] == close_char && depth > 0) {
                    depth--;
                    if (depth == 0) {
                        start = first_open;
                        end = i;
                        break;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    if (start == -1 || end == -1 || start >= end - 1) {
        printf("[DEBUG] No valid delimiters found!\n");
        return;
    }
    
    // Delete inside
    int delete_start = start + 1;
    int delete_end = end;
    
    printf("[DEBUG] Deleting from %d to %d (inside %c%c)\n", delete_start, delete_end, open_char, close_char);
    
    memmove(&text[delete_start], &text[delete_end], len - delete_end + 1);
    UI_SetText(g_ActiveEditControl, text);
    UI_Edit_SetSel(g_ActiveEditControl, delete_start, delete_start);
    
    if (keep_mode) {
        SetMode(MODE_INSERT);
    } else {
        SetMode(MODE_NORMAL);
    }
}

static inline void DeleteInnerWord(bool keep_mode) {
    if (!g_ActiveEditControl || !IsWindow(g_ActiveEditControl)) return;
    
    char text[512] = {0};
    UI_GetText(g_ActiveEditControl, text, sizeof(text));
    int len = (int)strlen(text);
    
    DWORD start_pos, end_pos;
    UI_Edit_GetSel(g_ActiveEditControl, &start_pos, &end_pos);
    int cursor = (int)start_pos;
    
    
    int word_start = cursor;
    while (word_start > 0) {
        char c = text[word_start - 1];
        if (!isalnum(c) && c != '_') break;
        word_start--;
    }
    
    int word_end = cursor;
    while (word_end < len) {
        char c = text[word_end];
        if (!isalnum(c) && c != '_') break;
        word_end++;
    }
    
    if (word_start == cursor && word_end == cursor) {
        word_end = cursor;
        while (word_end < len) {
            char c = text[word_end];
            if (isalnum(c) || c == '_') break;
            word_end++;
        }
        if (word_end >= len) return; // No word found
        word_start = word_end;
        while (word_start < len) {
            char c = text[word_start];
            if (!isalnum(c) && c != '_') break;
            word_start++;
        }
        int temp = word_start;
        word_start = cursor;
        word_end = temp;
    }
    
    if (word_start == cursor && word_end == cursor) {
        word_start = cursor;
        while (word_start > 0) {
            char c = text[word_start - 1];
            if (!isalnum(c) && c != '_') break;
            word_start--;
        }
        word_end = cursor;
        while (word_end < len) {
            char c = text[word_end];
            if (!isalnum(c) && c != '_') break;
            word_end++;
        }
    }
    
    if (word_start == cursor && word_end == cursor) {
        return;
    }
    
    
    if (word_start > word_end) {
        int temp = word_start;
        word_start = word_end;
        word_end = temp;
    }
    
    if (word_start >= 0 && word_end <= len && word_start < word_end) {
        int delete_len = word_end - word_start;
        memmove(&text[word_start], &text[word_end], len - word_end + 1);
        
        UI_SetText(g_ActiveEditControl, text);
        
        UI_Edit_SetSel(g_ActiveEditControl, word_start, word_start);
        
        if (keep_mode) {
            SetMode(MODE_INSERT);
        } else {
            SetMode(MODE_NORMAL);
        }
    }
}

typedef enum {
    VIM_SCOPE_ALL = 0,
    VIM_SCOPE_MODAL,
    VIM_SCOPE_POPUP,
} VimScope;

static inline bool ScopeMatches(VimScope scope, bool in_popup) {
    switch (scope) {
        case VIM_SCOPE_ALL:   return true;
        case VIM_SCOPE_MODAL: return !in_popup;
        case VIM_SCOPE_POPUP: return in_popup;
        default:              return false;
    }
}

    char buffer[256];
    if (selected_index >= 0 && selected_index < g_populated_count) {
        char text[512] = {0};

        text[sizeof(text) - 1] = '\0';

        return;

    }
}

        char buffer[256];
        UI_GetCurrentText(g_ActiveEditControl,textBuffer);
        
        textBuffer[strcspn(textBuffer, "\r\n")] = '\0';

        } else {
            printf("[VIM] Yank failed: Search box is currently empty.\n");
        }
    }
}

static inline void HandleMoveWordForward(void) {
    char text[512];
      
    UI_GetCurrentText(g_ActiveEditControl,text);
    
    DWORD start = 0;
    DWORD end = 0;

    UI_GetCurrentCursorPosition(g_ActiveEditControl, &start, &end);
    
    int pos = (int)start;
    int len = (int)strlen(text);

    if (len < (int)sizeof(text) - 1) {
        text[len] = ' ';
        text[len + 1] = '\0';
        len++; 
    }
    
    if (pos < len) {
      for (int i = pos + 1; i < len; i++) {
        if (text[i] != ' ') {
          continue;
        } else {
          for (int j = i; j < len; j++) {
            if (text[j] != ' ') {
              UI_SetCursorPosition(g_ActiveEditControl,j);
              break;
            } else {
              continue;
            }
          }
          break;
        }
      }
  }
}

static inline void HandleMoveWordBackward(void) {
    char text[512];
    UI_GetCurrentText(g_ActiveEditControl, text);
    
    DWORD start = 0;
    DWORD end = 0;
    UI_GetCurrentCursorPosition(g_ActiveEditControl, &start, &end);
    
    int pos = (int)start;

    for (int i = pos - 1; i >= -1; i--) {
      
      char current_char = (i >= 0) ? text[i] : ' ';

      if (current_char != ' ') {
        continue;
      } else {
        for (int j = i - 1; j >= -1; j--) {
          char next_char = (j >= 0) ? text[j] : ' ';

          if (next_char != ' ') {
            UI_SetCursorPosition(g_ActiveEditControl, j + 1);
            return; 
          } else {
            continue;
          }
        }
        
        UI_SetCursorPosition(g_ActiveEditControl, 0);
        return;
      }
    }
}

/* === FILE: volume.h === */
#ifndef VOLUME_H
#define VOLUME_H

#include <windows.h>
#include <mmdeviceapi.h>
#include <endpointvolume.h>
#include "config.h"
#include "types.h"
#include <stdlib.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <initguid.h>

DEFINE_GUID(CLSID_MMDeviceEnumerator, 0xbcde0395, 0xe52f, 0x467c, 0x8e, 0x3d, 0xc4, 0x57, 0x92, 0x91, 0x69, 0x2e);
DEFINE_GUID(IID_IMMDeviceEnumerator, 0xa95664d2, 0x9614, 0x4f35, 0xa7, 0x46, 0xde, 0x8d, 0xb6, 0x36, 0x17, 0xe6);
DEFINE_GUID(IID_IAudioEndpointVolume, 0x5cdf2c82, 0x841e, 0x4546, 0x97, 0x22, 0x0c, 0xf7, 0x40, 0x78, 0x22, 0x9a);

int GetSystemVolume() {
    HRESULT hr;
    IMMDeviceEnumerator *pEnumerator = NULL;
    IMMDevice *pDevice = NULL;
    IAudioEndpointVolume *pVolume = NULL;
    float currentVolume = 0.0f;

    hr = CoInitialize(NULL);
    if (FAILED(hr)) return 0;

    hr = CoCreateInstance(&CLSID_MMDeviceEnumerator, NULL, CLSCTX_ALL,
                          &IID_IMMDeviceEnumerator, (void**)&pEnumerator);
    if (FAILED(hr)) goto cleanup;

    hr = pEnumerator->lpVtbl->GetDefaultAudioEndpoint(pEnumerator, eRender, eConsole, &pDevice);
    if (FAILED(hr)) goto cleanup;

    hr = pDevice->lpVtbl->Activate(pDevice, &IID_IAudioEndpointVolume, CLSCTX_ALL, NULL,
                                   (void**)&pVolume);
    if (FAILED(hr)) goto cleanup;

    pVolume->lpVtbl->GetMasterVolumeLevelScalar(pVolume, &currentVolume);

cleanup:
    if (pVolume) pVolume->lpVtbl->Release(pVolume);
    if (pDevice) pDevice->lpVtbl->Release(pDevice);
    if (pEnumerator) pEnumerator->lpVtbl->Release(pEnumerator);
    CoUninitialize();

    return (int)(currentVolume * 100.0f + 0.5f); // Rounding up
}

void SetSystemVolume(float volume) {
    if (volume < 0.0f) volume = 0.0f;
    if (volume > 1.0f) volume = 1.0f;
    
    HRESULT hr;
    IMMDeviceEnumerator *pEnumerator = NULL;
    IMMDevice *pDevice = NULL;
    IAudioEndpointVolume *pVolume = NULL;

    hr = CoInitialize(NULL);
    if (FAILED(hr)) return;

    hr = CoCreateInstance(&CLSID_MMDeviceEnumerator, NULL, CLSCTX_ALL,
                          &IID_IMMDeviceEnumerator, (void**)&pEnumerator);
    if (FAILED(hr)) goto cleanup;

    hr = pEnumerator->lpVtbl->GetDefaultAudioEndpoint(pEnumerator, eRender, eConsole, &pDevice);
    if (FAILED(hr)) goto cleanup;

    hr = pDevice->lpVtbl->Activate(pDevice, &IID_IAudioEndpointVolume, CLSCTX_ALL, NULL,
                                   (void**)&pVolume);
    if (FAILED(hr)) goto cleanup;

    pVolume->lpVtbl->SetMasterVolumeLevelScalar(pVolume, volume, NULL);

cleanup:
    if (pVolume) pVolume->lpVtbl->Release(pVolume);
    if (pDevice) pDevice->lpVtbl->Release(pDevice);
    if (pEnumerator) pEnumerator->lpVtbl->Release(pEnumerator);
    CoUninitialize();
}


void TriggerVolume() {
    if (CheckAndBlockIfPopupActive(hPopupWnd)) return;
    int current_vol = GetSystemVolume();
    char prompt[256]; 
    char* input;
    snprintf(prompt, sizeof(prompt), "Enter Volume (0 - 100) Current Volume: %d", current_vol);
    
    input = SimplePromptUser(prompt, "🔊 Volume",920, 650, 32);
    if (input != NULL) {
    int vol = atoi(input);
    if (vol < 0) vol = 0;
    if (vol > 100) vol = 100;
    SetSystemVolume(vol / 100.0f);
    }
    
}

#endif 


/* === FILE: window_switcher.h === */
#ifndef WINDOW_SWITCHER_H
#define WINDOW_SWITCHER_H

#include <windows.h>
#include "types.h"
#include "focus_switcher.h"
#include <stdio.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <shlwapi.h>
#include <psapi.h>
#include "common_ui.h"

static SavedWindow g_switcher_windows[100];
static int g_switcher_count = 0;

typedef struct {
    DWORD processId;
    char exeName[64];
    DWORD lastAccessTime;
} ExeCacheEntry;

static ExeCacheEntry g_exeCache[128] = {0};
static int g_exeCacheCount = 0;
static DWORD g_lastCacheCleanup = 0;

static void FetchExeNameOnDemand(HWND hwnd, char* outExe, DWORD maxLen) {
    DWORD processId = 0;
    GetWindowThreadProcessId(hwnd, &processId);
    
    if (processId == 0) {
        strncpy(outExe, "Unknown.exe", maxLen);
        return;
    }
    
    DWORD currentTime = GetTickCount();
    
    if (currentTime - g_lastCacheCleanup > 10000) {
        g_lastCacheCleanup = currentTime;
        int keepCount = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < g_exeCacheCount; i++) {
            if (currentTime - g_exeCache[i].lastAccessTime < 5000) {
                if (keepCount != i) {
                    memcpy(&g_exeCache[keepCount], &g_exeCache[i], sizeof(ExeCacheEntry));
                }
                keepCount++;
            }
        }
        g_exeCacheCount = keepCount;
    }
    
    for (int i = 0; i < g_exeCacheCount; i++) {
        if (g_exeCache[i].processId == processId) {
            g_exeCache[i].lastAccessTime = currentTime;
            strncpy(outExe, g_exeCache[i].exeName, maxLen);
            return;
        }
    }
    
    HANDLE hProcess = OpenProcess(PROCESS_QUERY_LIMITED_INFORMATION, FALSE, processId);
    if (hProcess) {
        char path[MAX_PATH] = {0};
        if (GetProcessImageFileNameA(hProcess, path, sizeof(path))) {
            char* lastBackslash = strrchr(path, '\\');
            if (lastBackslash) {
                strncpy(outExe, lastBackslash + 1, maxLen);
            } else {
                strncpy(outExe, path, maxLen);
            }
        } else {
            strncpy(outExe, "Unknown.exe", maxLen);
        }
        CloseHandle(hProcess);
    } else {
        strncpy(outExe, "Unknown.exe", maxLen);
    }
    
    if (g_exeCacheCount < 128) {
        g_exeCache[g_exeCacheCount].processId = processId;
        strncpy(g_exeCache[g_exeCacheCount].exeName, outExe, sizeof(g_exeCache[0].exeName) - 1);
        g_exeCache[g_exeCacheCount].lastAccessTime = currentTime;
        g_exeCacheCount++;
    }
}

static bool switcher_match_helper(const char* item, const char* filter) {
    for (int i = 0; i < g_switcher_count; i++) {
        if (g_switcher_windows[i].title == item) {
            return (StrStrIA(g_switcher_windows[i].title, filter) != NULL ||
                    StrStrIA(g_switcher_windows[i].exe, filter) != NULL);
        }
    }
    return false;
}

static void switcher_refresh_callback(const char* filter, HWND hListBox) {
    if (!hListBox) return;
    
    const char* titles[100];
    int count = (g_switcher_count < 100) ? g_switcher_count : 100;
    
    for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        titles[i] = g_switcher_windows[i].title;
    }
    
    PopulateListBox(hListBox, filter, titles, count, switcher_match_helper);
}

static void switcher_enter_callback(const char* selection) {
    if (selection == NULL || strlen(selection) == 0) return;
    
    for (int i = 0; i < g_switcher_count; i++) {
        if (StrStrIA(g_switcher_windows[i].title, selection) != NULL) {
            HWND target_hwnd = g_switcher_windows[i].hwnd;
            if (IsWindow(target_hwnd)) {
                FocusWindow(target_hwnd);
                printf("[SWITCHER] Focused: %s\n", g_switcher_windows[i].title);
            }
            break;
        }
    }
}

static void TriggerWindowSwitcher() {
    if (hPopupWnd != NULL && IsWindow(hPopupWnd)) {
        return;
    }
    WindowList myWindows;
    myWindows.count = 0;
    myWindows.capacity = 10;
    myWindows.items = (WindowData*)malloc(myWindows.capacity * sizeof(WindowData));
    if (myWindows.items != NULL) {
        EnumWindows(EnumWindowsProc, (LPARAM)&myWindows);
    }

    char *list_of_not_wanteds[] = {"Input", "Manager", "Settings"};
    size_t size_of_list = sizeof(list_of_not_wanteds) / sizeof(list_of_not_wanteds[0]);

    g_switcher_count = 0;

    for (int i = 0; i < myWindows.count; i++) {
        HWND targetHwnd = myWindows.items[i].hwnd;
        char* targetTitle = myWindows.items[i].title;
        bool not_wanted = false;
        
        for (size_t j = 0; j < size_of_list; j++) {
            if (strstr(targetTitle, list_of_not_wanteds[j]) != NULL) {
                not_wanted = true;
                break; 
            }
        }
        if (not_wanted) continue; 

        g_switcher_windows[g_switcher_count].index = g_switcher_count;
        g_switcher_windows[g_switcher_count].hwnd = targetHwnd;
        strncpy(g_switcher_windows[g_switcher_count].title, targetTitle, sizeof(g_switcher_windows[g_switcher_count].title) - 1);
        g_switcher_windows[g_switcher_count].title[sizeof(g_switcher_windows[g_switcher_count].title) - 1] = '\0'; 

        FetchExeNameOnDemand(targetHwnd, g_switcher_windows[g_switcher_count].exe, sizeof(g_switcher_windows[g_switcher_count].exe) - 1);
        g_switcher_count++;
        if (g_switcher_count >= 100) break;
    }

    printf("[SWITCHER] Filter complete. Kept %d windows out of %d.\n", g_switcher_count, myWindows.count);

    if (g_switcher_count == 0) {
        printf("[SWITCHER] No windows passed the filter.\n");
        return;
    }

    const char* ui_list[100];
    for (int i = 0; i < g_switcher_count; i++) {
        ui_list[i] = g_switcher_windows[i].title;
    }

    printf("[SWITCHER] Launching UI Selection Prompt...\n");
    char* selected_title = ImportantPromptUser(ui_list,"🪟 Switcher",g_switcher_count, 920, 650,32,
                                                switcher_refresh_callback,  
                                                switcher_enter_callback,    
                                                NULL);

    free(myWindows.items);
}

#endif


/* === FILE: yazi_zoxide.h === */
#ifndef YAZI_ZOXIDE_H
#define YAZI_ZOXIDE_H

#include <windows.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include "types.h"
#include "helpers.h"
#include "config.h"

#define FILE_YAZI_ZOXIDE 1
#define YaziZoxideFile G_FILE_ENTRIES[FILE_YAZI_ZOXIDE].mf

static char** g_cached_yazi_commands = NULL;
static int g_cached_yazi_count = 0;

static void RebuildYaziCache(void) {
    if (g_cached_yazi_commands) {
        for (int i = 0; i < g_cached_yazi_count; i++) {
            free(g_cached_yazi_commands[i]);
        }
        free(g_cached_yazi_commands);
        g_cached_yazi_commands = NULL;
        g_cached_yazi_count = 0;
    }
    
    if (!YaziZoxideFile || YaziZoxideFile->fileSize == 0) {
        return;
    }
    
    size_t lineCount = 0;
    Line* lines = FileGetLines(YaziZoxideFile, &lineCount);
    if (!lines) return;
    
    g_cached_yazi_commands = (char**)malloc(lineCount * sizeof(char*));
    g_cached_yazi_count = 0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < lineCount; i++) {
        size_t len = lines[i].length;
        while (len > 0 && lines[i].ptr[len-1] == '\r') len--;
        
        if (len > 0) {
            char* cmd = (char*)malloc(len + 1);
            memcpy(cmd, lines[i].ptr, len);
            cmd[len] = '\0';
            g_cached_yazi_commands[g_cached_yazi_count++] = cmd;
        }
    }
    
    free(lines);
}


static void yazi_refresh_callback(const char* filter, HWND hListBox) {
    if (!hListBox) return;
    
    if (!g_cached_yazi_commands) {
        RebuildYaziCache();
    }
    
    PopulateListBox(hListBox, filter, (const char**)g_cached_yazi_commands, g_cached_yazi_count, NULL);
}


static void yazi_enter_callback(const char* selection) {
    int selected_index = LB_ERR;
    if (hListBox != NULL) {
        selected_index = UI_GetSelection(hListBox);
    }
    
    if (selected_index == LB_ERR) {
        char input[256] = {0};
        if (hEditControl != NULL) {
            UI_GetText(hEditControl, input, sizeof(input));
            if (strlen(input) > 0) {
                FileAppend(YaziZoxideFile, input);
                FileAppend(YaziZoxideFile, "\n");
                RebuildYaziCache();
                
                char* path = QueryZoxidePath(input);
                if (path) {
                    char cmd_args[512];
                    snprintf(cmd_args, sizeof(cmd_args), "-e yazi \"%s\"", path);
                    char terminal[512];
                    snprintf(terminal,sizeof(terminal),"%s.exe",UsedTerminal);
                    ShellExecuteA(NULL, "open", terminal, cmd_args, NULL, SW_SHOWNORMAL);
                }
            }
        }
    } else {
        char* path = QueryZoxidePath(selection);
        if (path) {
            char cmd_args[512];
            snprintf(cmd_args, sizeof(cmd_args), "-e yazi \"%s\"", path);
            char terminal[512];
            snprintf(terminal,sizeof(terminal),"%s.exe",UsedTerminal);
            ShellExecuteA(NULL, "open", terminal, cmd_args, NULL, SW_SHOWNORMAL);
        }
    }
}
static void yazi_edit_callback(const char* selection) {
    char* NewyaziQuery = EditTextPopup(selection,960,540,1000,300);
    
    if (NewyaziQuery != NULL) {
        WriteToCacheIndex(g_SelectedIndex,NewyaziQuery);
    }
    
    ReplaceLineInMappedFile(YaziZoxideFile,g_SelectedIndex,NewyaziQuery);
    
    RebuildYaziCache();
    
    yazi_refresh_callback("",hListBox);
}

static void TriggerYaziZoxide() {
    if (CheckAndBlockIfPopupActive(hPopupWnd)) return;
    
    if (!g_cached_yazi_commands) {
        RebuildYaziCache();
    }
    
    const char** list = (const char**)malloc(g_cached_yazi_count * sizeof(char*));
    if (!list) return;
    
    for (int i = 0; i < g_cached_yazi_count; i++) {
        list[i] = g_cached_yazi_commands[i];
    }
    
    ImportantPromptUser(list, "📂 Yazi", g_cached_yazi_count, 920, 650, 32, 
               yazi_refresh_callback, 
               yazi_enter_callback, 
               yazi_edit_callback);
    
    free(list);
}

#endif


/* === FILE: zoxide.h === */
#ifndef ZOXIDE_H
#define ZOXIDE_H

#include <windows.h>
#include <stdio.h>
#include <string.h>
#include <stdlib.h>
#include "types.h"
#include "helpers.h"
#include "config.h"

#define FILE_TERMINAL_ZOXIDE 3
#define TerminalZoxideFile G_FILE_ENTRIES[FILE_TERMINAL_ZOXIDE].mf

static char** g_cached_zoxide_commands = NULL;
static int g_cached_zoxide_count = 0;

static void RebuildZoxideCache(void) {
    if (g_cached_zoxide_commands) {
        for (int i = 0; i < g_cached_zoxide_count; i++) {
            free(g_cached_zoxide_commands[i]);
        }
        free(g_cached_zoxide_commands);
        g_cached_zoxide_commands = NULL;
        g_cached_zoxide_count = 0;
    }
    
    if (!TerminalZoxideFile || TerminalZoxideFile->fileSize == 0) {
        return;
    }
    
    size_t lineCount = 0;
    Line* lines = FileGetLines(TerminalZoxideFile, &lineCount);
    if (!lines) return;
    
    g_cached_zoxide_commands = (char**)malloc(lineCount * sizeof(char*));
    g_cached_zoxide_count = 0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < lineCount; i++) {
        size_t len = lines[i].length;
        while (len > 0 && lines[i].ptr[len-1] == '\r') len--;
        
        if (len > 0) {
            char* cmd = (char*)malloc(len + 1);
            memcpy(cmd, lines[i].ptr, len);
            cmd[len] = '\0';
            g_cached_zoxide_commands[g_cached_zoxide_count++] = cmd;
        }
    }
    
    free(lines);
}

static void zoxide_refresh_callback(const char* filter, HWND hListBox) {
    if (!hListBox) return;
    
    if (!g_cached_zoxide_commands) {
        RebuildZoxideCache();
    }
    
    PopulateListBox(hListBox, filter, (const char**)g_cached_zoxide_commands, g_cached_zoxide_count, NULL);
}

static void zoxide_edit_callback(const char* selection) {
    char* NewZoxideQuery = EditTextPopup(selection,960,540,1000,300);
    
    if (NewZoxideQuery != NULL) {
        WriteToCacheIndex(g_SelectedIndex,NewZoxideQuery);
    }
    
    ReplaceLineInMappedFile(TerminalZoxideFile,g_SelectedIndex,NewZoxideQuery);
    
    RebuildZoxideCache();
    
    zoxide_refresh_callback("",hListBox);
}



static void zoxide_enter_callback(const char* selection) {
    int selected_index = LB_ERR;
    if (hListBox != NULL) {
        selected_index = UI_GetSelection(hListBox);
    }
    
    if (selected_index == LB_ERR) {
        char input[256] = {0};
        if (hEditControl != NULL) {
            UI_GetText(hEditControl, input, sizeof(input));
            printf("THE USER INPUT  IS %s",input);
            if (strlen(input) > 0) {
                if (TerminalZoxideFile->fileSize > 0) {
                    char lastChar = TerminalZoxideFile->pBuffer[TerminalZoxideFile->fileSize - 1];
                    if (lastChar != '\n') {
                        FileAppend(TerminalZoxideFile, "\n");
                    }
                }

                FileAppend(TerminalZoxideFile, input);
                FileAppend(TerminalZoxideFile, "\n");
                RebuildZoxideCache();
                
                
                char* path = QueryZoxidePath(input);
                if (path != NULL) {
                    char terminal[512];
                    snprintf(terminal,sizeof(terminal),"%s.exe",UsedTerminal);
                    ShellExecuteA(NULL, "open", terminal, NULL, path, SW_SHOWNORMAL);
                }
            }
        }
    } else {
        char* path = QueryZoxidePath(selection);
        if (path != NULL) {
            char terminal[512];
            snprintf(terminal,sizeof(terminal),"%s.exe",UsedTerminal);
            ShellExecuteA(NULL, "open", terminal, NULL, path, SW_SHOWNORMAL);
        }
    }
}

static void TriggerZoxide() {
    if (CheckAndBlockIfPopupActive(hPopupWnd)) return;
    
    if (!g_cached_zoxide_commands) {
        RebuildZoxideCache();
    }
    
    const char** list = (const char**)malloc(g_cached_zoxide_count * sizeof(char*));
    if (!list) return;
    
    for (int i = 0; i < g_cached_zoxide_count; i++) {
        list[i] = g_cached_zoxide_commands[i];
    }
    
    ImportantPromptUser(list, "⌨️ Zoxide", g_cached_zoxide_count,920,650, 32, 
               zoxide_refresh_callback, 
               zoxide_enter_callback, 
               zoxide_edit_callback);
    
    free(list);
}
